TL;DR: 本文通过构建缺失的对比实验，发现强化学习中模型合并（TIES、RAM+）在多任务目标完成率上与联合多任务训练效果相当，且不同合并方法之间无显著差异。原因在于专家任务向量接近正交（余弦相似度0.06-0.10），导致基于支撑集和符号的合并方法退化为均匀平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并被宣传为联合多任务训练的替代方案，但在强化学习设置中，这种替代关系从未经过系统验证——因为人们合并独立发布的智能体恰恰是因为联合模型不可用。本文旨在填补这一缺失的基准对比。

Method: 训练难度-1和难度-2的Qwen3-8B专家智能体（在AppWorld智能体基准上使用LOOP训练），分别使用TIES和RAM+进行合并，然后与在同一数据上联合训练的模型进行对比评估。同时测量专家任务向量的几何特性（余弦相似度、支撑集重叠度），并与随机初始化基线和同运行上限进行校准。

Result: 在任务目标完成率上，模型合并的效果与联合RL训练相当，所有合并变体之间统计上无显著差异。专家任务向量接近正交（余弦相似度0.06-0.10），尽管支撑集重叠度约为65%。由于方向和支撑集解耦，基于支撑集和符号的合并方法（RAM、TIES）退化为接近均匀平均。

Conclusion: 模型合并在RL多任务场景中确实可以作为联合训练的可行替代方案；但合并方法的选择不重要，因为任务向量正交性导致不同方法趋同。研究揭示了任务向量几何特性对合并效果的决定性影响。

Abstract: Model merging is promoted as a substitute for joint multi-task training, yet in the reinforcement-learning setting this substitution is essentially never tested against the baseline it claims to replace: methods merge independently released agents precisely because a joint model is unavailable. We build the missing comparison. Training difficulty-1 and difficulty-2 Qwen3-8B specialists on the AppWorld agent benchmark with LOOP, we merge them (TIES, RAM+) and pit the result against a jointly trained model on the same data. On task-goal completion, merging matches joint RL -- and every merge variant is statistically indistinguishable. To explain why merge method does not matter here, we measure the geometry of the specialists' task vectors, which carries no task-sampling noise: they are near-orthogonal (cosine 0.06 - 0.10) despite ~65% support overlap, a small, shared direction that grows over training and that we calibrate against a random-init floor and a same-run ceiling to confirm it reflects learning, not the low-rank parameterization. Because direction and support are decoupled, support and sign-based merging (RAM, TIES) collapse to near-uniform averaging. We release all code and statistics.

</details>


### [49] [A Semiparametric Framework for Stochastic Fundamental Diagram Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15907)
*Pengnan Chi,Xiaoliang Ma,Magnus Jansson,Magnus Nordenvaad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种半参数随机基本图(SFD)建模框架，通过特殊设计的函数形式内在满足物理约束（密度-流量条件矩），同时利用神经网络捕捉复杂非线性模式。推导了矩匹配方程组将物理约束转化为条件分布参数化，并证明位置-尺度分布族存在唯一解。在真实数据集上优于基线方法，尤其在拥堵状态具有更优的概率预测与不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机基本图模型难以同时满足严格的物理约束（如密度-流量的单调性、边界条件）并保持足够的灵活性以拟合复杂非线性模式，亟需一种既能保证理论严谨性又能实现高表达力的建模框架。

Method: 1) 设计特殊函数形式，内在满足条件流量分布矩的物理约束；2) 引入神经网络结构增强对经验数据中复杂非线性模式的拟合能力；3) 推导矩匹配方程组，将物理约束转化为条件分布的参数化问题，并证明位置-尺度分布族解的存在唯一性；4) 扩展到非位置-尺度分布（含边界约束）。

Result: 在真实交通数据集上，所提方法在概率预测精度和不确定性量化方面持续优于代表性基线，尤其在拥堵交通状态下表现更为突出。

Conclusion: 该半参数SFD框架为随机交通流建模提供了理论严谨且灵活的基础，兼顾了物理可解释性与数据驱动表达力。

Abstract: The stochastic fundamental diagram (SFD) provides a probabilistic description of the relationship between traffic density and flow or speed, enabling uncertainty-aware traffic modeling. However, existing stochastic models frequently struggle to accommodate rigorous physical constraints while retaining sufficient flexibility to capture complex nonlinear patterns. To address this, we propose a novel semiparametric SFD modeling framework by leveraging specially designed functional forms. These functions intrinsically satisfy physical constraints defined on the moments of the conditional flow distribution given traffic density while incorporating neural-network-based structures to capture complex empirical patterns. We derive a system of moment-matching equations to convert physical constraints into the parameterization of the conditional distribution, proving that a unique solution exists for the location-scale family of distributions, thereby guaranteeing model well-posedness. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the framework can be extended to non-location-scale distributions, including those requiring additional boundary constraints. Empirical evaluations on a real-world dataset reveal that our approach consistently outperforms representative baselines, delivering superior probabilistic accuracy and robust uncertainty quantification, particularly in congested regimes. Overall, the proposed framework provides a theoretically grounded and flexible foundation for stochastic traffic flow modeling.

</details>


### [50] [DADiff: Diffusion-Driven Cross-Domain Policy Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16090)
*Hanyang Chen,Anirudh Satheesh,Longchao Da,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DADiff，一种基于扩散模型的跨域强化学习策略迁移框架，通过生成轨迹差异估计动力学不匹配，并开发了奖励修改与数据选择两种变体来适应目标域，实验证明其在多种环境变化下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中源域与目标域之间的动力学不匹配导致策略迁移困难，现有方法（如域分类器、价值引导过滤、表示学习）存在局限，因此需要一种新的生成建模视角来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出DADiff框架，利用扩散模型在下一状态生成过程中比较源域与目标域的生成轨迹差异来估计动力学不匹配，并开发了奖励修改和数据选择两种变体来适应目标域策略。

Result: 在多种环境变化下进行实验，结果表明DADiff在解决动力学不匹配问题上优于现有方法，并验证了理论分析中性能差异上界与生成轨迹偏差的关系。

Conclusion: 基于扩散模型的生成式方法能够有效估计和缓解跨域动力学不匹配，奖励修改与数据选择两种变体各有适用场景，理论分析为方法提供了可靠性保证。

Abstract: Transferring policies across domains poses a vital challenge in reinforcement learning, due to the dynamics mismatch between the source and target domains. In this paper, we consider the setting of online dynamics adaptation, where policies are trained in the source domain with sufficient data, while only limited interactions with the target domain are allowed. There are a few existing works that address the dynamics mismatch by employing domain classifiers, value-guided data filtering, or representation learning. Instead, we study the domain adaptation problem from a generative modeling perspective. Specifically, we introduce DADiff, a diffusion-based framework that leverages the discrepancy between source and target domain generative trajectories in the generation process of the next state to estimate the dynamics mismatch. Both reward modification and data selection variants are developed to adapt the policy to the target domain. We also provide a theoretical analysis to show that the performance difference of a given policy between the two domains is bounded by the generative trajectory deviation. More discussions on the applicability of the variants and the connection between our theoretical analysis and the prior work are further provided. We conduct extensive experiments in environments with various shifts to validate the effectiveness of our method. The results demonstrate that our method provides superior performance compared to existing approaches, effectively addressing the dynamics mismatch. We provide the code of our method at https://github.com/hanyang-chen/DADiff-release

</details>


### [51] [(MPO)$^2$: Multivariate Polynomial Optimization based on Matrix Product Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15916)
*Niccolò Ciolli,Anders Vestergaard Nørskov,Michael Kastoryano,Petr Taborsky,Morten Mørup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出(MPO)²框架，基于矩阵乘积算子(MPO)实现特征嵌入与紧凑多项式权重张量的联合学习，用于高效的多项式函数逼近，解决了现有张量分解多项式模型秩受限和特征顺序依赖的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式模型的系数张量随度数指数增长，现有张量化方法中，CP分解存在秩受限的表达能力问题，张量列(TC)形式则依赖特征顺序，限制了模型的灵活性和效率。

Method: 提出(MPO)²框架，将学习的MPO特征嵌入与紧凑多项式权重张量结合，实现特征顺序无关的多项式表示，并能融入投影、卷积、掩码等结构化算子以施加权重张量对称性约束。

Result: 在回归和分类基准上，(MPO)²优于现有的基于张量分解的多项式模型，提供了灵活且高效的多项式函数逼近替代方案。

Conclusion: (MPO)²有效解决了多项式模型的关键局限，为机器学习中复杂输入-输出关系的学习提供了一种更优的张量化多项式建模框架。

Abstract: Central to machine learning and signal processing is the ability to perform universal function approximation and learn complex input-output relationships from limited numbers of observations. Multivariate polynomial models offer a natural way to express such relationships through multiplicative feature interactions, but their coefficient tensors grow exponentially in size with the polynomial degree. Existing tensorized polynomial models reduce this cost, yet canonical polyadic decompositions have rank-limited expressivity, and tensor train formulations are feature order dependent. We introduce Multivariate Polynomial Optimization based on Matrix Product Operators (MPO)$^2$, a framework that combines learned MPO feature embeddings with compact polynomial weight tensors. This yields feature order independent polynomial representations that can incorporate structured operators such as projections, convolutions, and masks for weight tensor symmetries. Across regression and classification benchmarks, (MPO)$^2$ improves over existing tensor decomposition based polynomial models and provides a flexible alternative for efficient polynomial function approximation.

</details>


### [52] [Understanding Reasoning from Pretraining to Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16097)
*Jingyan Shen,Ang Li,Salman Rahman,Yifan Sun,Micah Goldblum,Matus Telgarsky,Pavel Izmailov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过国际象棋作为受控实验平台，系统研究了预训练（模型大小、数据）如何影响后续强化学习（RL）后训练的性能回报，并揭示了RL对模型的具体作用机制。研究发现：后RL性能可由预训练损失良好预测，RL奖励曲线斜率随预训练token数近似线性提升；RL并非简单锐化SFT策略，而是在简单问题上放大已有正确动作，在困难问题上激活SFT下几乎不存在的正确动作。这些发现也在数学领域文本上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将RL后训练与预训练阶段孤立开来，导致两个基本问题未解：(1)预训练选择（模型大小、数据）如何塑造RL计算回报；(2)RL实际上对模型做了什么。标准LLM设置中预训练语料庞大且不可控，难以归因行为来自预训练还是RL，且系统性计算扫描成本过高。

Method: 使用国际象棋作为受控测试平台，遵循标准LLM训练流水线：在人类棋谱上预训练5M到1B参数的语言模型，在合成推理轨迹上监督微调（SFT），然后在具有可验证奖励的国际象棋谜题上运行RL。

Result: (1)给定RL计算水平下的后RL性能可由预训练损失良好预测；(2)RL奖励曲线斜率随预训练token数近似线性提升；(3)RL在简单问题上放大SFT已偏好的正确动作，在困难问题上激活SFT下几乎不存在的正确动作；(4)在数学领域文本上，更长时间预训练的检查点达到更高的后RL性能且RL下提升更快。

Conclusion: 提供了预训练到RL接口的定量描述，以及一个用于研究完整预训练到后训练流水线中推理科学的受控测试平台。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to improving large language models (LLMs) on complex reasoning tasks, yet RL post-training is largely studied in isolation from the pretraining that precedes it. As a result, two basic questions remain open: (1) how do pretraining choices (model size, data) shape the returns to RL compute, and (2) what does RL actually do to the model? These questions are difficult to study in the standard LLM setting: pretraining corpora are vast and uncontrolled, making it hard to attribute behaviors to pretraining versus RL, and systematic compute sweeps across both stages are prohibitively expensive. To address these challenges, we use chess as a controlled testbed for studying reasoning across the full pretraining-to-post-training pipeline. We follow the standard LLM training pipeline by pretraining language models from 5M to 1B parameters on human chess games, supervised fine-tuning on synthetic reasoning traces, and running RL on chess puzzles with verifiable rewards. Using this framework, we find that the post-RL performance at given RL compute level is well-predicted from the pretraining loss, and slope of the RL reward curves improves approximately linearly with the pretraining tokens. Beyond scaling, we find that RL does not simply sharpen the SFT policy: on easy puzzles it amplifies correct moves the SFT policy already preferred, while on hard puzzles it surfaces correct moves that were nearly absent under SFT. We further test whether our findings transfer beyond chess by training a 1B language model on math-domain text, where the same predictive pattern emerges: longer-pretrained checkpoints reach higher post-RL performance and improve faster under RL. In sum, we provide a quantitative account of the pretraining-to-RL interface and a controlled testbed for studying the science of reasoning across the full pretraining-to-post-training pipeline.

</details>


### [53] [When Do Multi-Agent Systems Help? An Information Bottleneck Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16133)
*Wendi Yu,Lianhao Zhou,Xiangjue Dong,Sai Sudarshan Barath,Declan Staunton,Byung-Jun Yoon,Xiaoning Qian,James Caverlee,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息瓶颈（Information Bottleneck）视角解释了多智能体系统（MAS）相对于单智能体系统（SAS）的优势来源：MAS的优势源于有界中继（bounded relay）下上下文压缩带来的效率提升与信息损失之间的权衡，受参数β控制。实验表明，当中继接近充足时，MAS对弱模型帮助更大；当信息损失显著时，MAS优势减弱甚至逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对MAS相较于SAS的优势缺乏统一理论解释，两者性能在不同设置下表现不一致，需要从根本原理上阐明MAS何时有效、何时无效。

Method: 提出信息瓶颈视角：SAS将完整推理轨迹共享在同一上下文中，MAS则通过有界中继消息连接隔离的局部上下文。形式化中继带宽约束下的压缩-信息损失权衡，引入有效参数β控制这一权衡。在5个基准、3种模型规模上进行了18项对照实验验证。

Result: 1) 无限中继带宽下，MAS可模拟SAS（传递完整上游上下文）；2) 有界中继下，上下文压缩在提升效率的同时可能损失任务相关信息；3) MAS优势在中继接近充足时显著，尤其对弱模型；4) 强模型能从冗余上下文中提取有用信息，压缩带来的增益小，MAS优势减弱或逆转。

Conclusion: 多智能体设计本质上是一个信息瓶颈优化问题，该视角解释了有界智能体间通信何时有益、何时有害，为MAS的设计原则提供了理论基础。

Abstract: LLM powered multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising paradigm for complex tasks. However, their advantages over single-agent systems (SAS) remain unclear, with performance varying inconsistently across settings. Here, we provide an information bottleneck perspective on elucidating the differences between MAS and SAS. Specifically, our key observation is that a SAS accumulates its full reasoning trace in one shared context, while a MAS uses isolated local contexts connected by bounded relay messages. We show that, under infinite relay bandwidth, any SAS can be simulated by a MAS that transmits the full upstream context. Thus, the nontrivial advantage of MAS arises under bounded relays, where compression introduces a fundamental trade-off: reducing redundant context can improve efficiency, but may also incur loss of task-relevant information. We formalize this trade-off as an information bottleneck controlled by an effective parameter $β$, which captures how the balance shifts with model capability, and shows that MAS gains arise when context reduction outweighs relay information loss. We conduct 18 controlled experiments across five benchmarks and three model scales to validate our theoretical studies. We observe that MAS consistently helps when relays are near-sufficient, especially for weaker models. In contrast, MAS gains shrink or reverse when relays incur information loss, especially for stronger models that can already extract useful information from redundant context and thus gain little from compression. Our study shows that multi-agent design is fundamentally an information-bottleneck optimization problem. This perspective explains when bounded inter-agent communication helps or hurts.

</details>


### [54] [Knowledge-Guided Cross-Modal Fusion for Adult-to-Pediatric ECG Transfer via Label-Conditioned Contrastive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15928)
*Xinran Liu,Yuwen Li,Hongxiang Gao,Heyang Xu,Jianqing Li,Zongmin Wang,Chengyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEACE是一种知识引导的多模态ECG框架，通过在成人MIMIC-IV上预训练，利用轴描述、标签查询网络、标签集对比学习和课程自适应融合，实现从成人到儿童ECG的有效迁移，在零样本、少样本和全微调下均显著提升儿童ECG诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成人ECG预训练模型在儿童ECG上迁移表现差，因为儿童标签稀缺且现有全局对齐方法将共现诊断的证据纠缠在一起，限制了跨域迁移能力。

Method: PEACE包含：(1) 按节律、形态、ST-T轴描述每个诊断并组成轴令牌；(2) 标签查询网络(LQN)以诊断标签为查询跨模态关注ECG令牌和轴令牌；(3) 标签集感知双向对比学习(LSBC)对齐共享诊断的ECG特征与融合嵌入；(4) 课程自适应融合(CAF)根据分类损失和训练进度控制对齐强度。

Result: 在ZZU-pECG上零样本AUC 59.39%，50-shot AUC 81.74%，全微调AUC 91.56%；在PTB-XL上9个标签微调达到96.90%宏平均AUC。消融实验证实标签条件知识对齐而非全局文本融合是儿童迁移的关键。

Conclusion: PEACE通过标签感知的知识对齐有效弥合成人与儿童ECG间的跨域差距，在标签稀缺场景下显著优于基线方法。

Abstract: Adult and pediatric electrocardiogram (ECG) interpretation relies on age-sensitive criteria, and models pretrained mainly on adult ECGs often transfer poorly to pediatric populations when pediatric labels are scarce. Existing multimodal ECG--text methods typically align waveforms and text at the global sample level, entangling evidence from co-occurring diagnoses and limiting transfer under this gap. We propose Pediatric-Adult ECG Alignment via Cross-modal Enhancement (PEACE), a knowledge-guided framework pretrained on the largely adult MIMIC-IV ECG corpus. PEACE describes each diagnosis along rhythm, morphology, and ST--T axes and, per recording, composes only positive-label descriptors into three axis tokens and a fused embedding. A label query network (LQN) uses diagnostic labels as queries to cross-attend over ECG tokens and axis tokens, while label set aware bidirectional contrastive learning (LSBC) aligns pooled ECG features with the fused embedding when recordings share diagnoses. Curriculum adaptive fusion (CAF) gates alignment strength according to smoothed classification loss and training progress, limiting disruption during early optimization. The knowledge branch is used only for training supervision; inference uses ECG signals alone. On ZZU-pECG, PEACE reaches macro average AUCs of 59.39%, 81.74%, and 91.56% under zero-shot, 50-shot, and full fine-tuning, with the clearest gains over foundation and knowledge-pretraining baselines under limited supervision; versus domain adaptation initializations, zero-shot improves substantially while 50-shot AUC is comparable to DANN. After fine-tuning on PTB-XL, PEACE reaches 96.90% macro average AUC over nine harmonized labels. Ablations confirm that label-conditioned knowledge alignment, rather than global text fusion, is the key driver of pediatric transfer gains.

</details>


### [55] [When Does Muon Help Agentic Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16169)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Zihe Huang,Ziqi Zhou,Qianshan Wei,Xuan Wang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了Muon优化器在基于强化学习的智能体后训练中的应用，发现仅对隐藏权重矩阵应用Muon（配合GiGPO）可将ALFWorld验证成功率从0.290提升至0.546（+88%），且效果依赖于优势估计器和学习率的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在大规模预训练中与AdamW竞争力相当，但其在强化学习（RL）后训练中的价值尚不明确，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct模型，在ALFWorld环境下通过稀疏奖励的智能体RL进行单种子匹配对比实验，采用Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (GiGPO)框架，仅对隐藏权重矩阵应用Muon优化器，并与AdamW对照。

Result: 在GiGPO下，Muon使最终窗口验证成功率从0.290提升至0.546（+88%），高学习率AdamW对照组无后更新成功。Muon在3e-5学习率下将GRPO从0.161提升至0.268；在1e-5学习率下，GraphGPO Muon达到0.901成功率，归一化验证AUC从0.399提升至0.556，达到0.5和0.75成功率的时间分别提前30和60个更新。

Conclusion: 探索性结果表明Muon能够提升智能体RL的性能，提示需要联合研究策略优化器、优势估计器和学习率的选择。多种子和跨任务验证仍待进一步研究。

Abstract: Muon is competitive with AdamW in large-scale pre-training, but its value for reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training remains unclear. We study vanilla Muon in sparse-reward agentic RL through matched single-seed comparisons with AdamW on ALFWorld using Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct. Under Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (GiGPO), applying Muon only to hidden weight matrices raises final-window validation success from 0.290 to 0.546 (+88%); high-rate AdamW controls retain no post-update success. The effect depends on the advantage estimator and learning rate. At 3e-5, Muon improves GRPO from 0.161 to 0.268, whereas GraphGPO's late-window gap narrows near saturation. At 1e-5, GraphGPO Muon reaches 0.901, raises normalized validation AUC from 0.399 to 0.556, and reaches 0.5 and 0.75 success 30 and 60 updates earlier, respectively. These exploratory results show that Muon can benefit agentic RL and motivate studying the policy optimizer, advantage estimator, and learning rate jointly. Multi-seed and cross-task validation remain open.

</details>


### [56] [An Exploratory Study of Single Channel Surface Electromyography for Hand Gesture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15972)
*Daanish Hindustani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索了使用单通道表面肌电信号(sEMG)结合轻量级机器学习架构实现十种手势分类的可行性。通过提取时域、频域、高阶过零和相对强度特征，结合皮尔逊相关性过滤和降维技术，使用前馈神经网络、KNN和SVM三种分类器进行系统评估。结果表明，结合时频特征与皮尔逊过滤的紧凑神经网络在有限时空信息下可达90%准确率，证明了单通道sEMG系统在低成本、低功耗手势识别应用中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多通道sEMG手势识别依赖大量传感器阵列和计算密集型模型，限制了其在低功耗和嵌入式系统中的实际部署。本研究旨在探索使用单通道sEMG结合轻量级机器学习方法实现高效手势识别的可行性，以降低成本与计算开销。

Method: 将原始sEMG信号转换为包含时域、频域、高阶过零和相对强度特征的综合特征表示；使用皮尔逊相关性过滤移除高度相关特征以减少冗余；分别应用LDA和PCA进行降维；系统评估了前馈神经网络(NN)、K近邻(KNN)和支持向量机(SVM)三种分类器在四个实验中的表现。

Result: 结合时域和频域特征、皮尔逊过滤以及紧凑神经网络，在有限的时空信息条件下可实现高达90%的分类准确率。

Conclusion: 单通道sEMG系统结合轻量级机器学习方法在低成本、低功耗手势识别应用中具有显著潜力，为实际嵌入式部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate hand gesture recognition using surface electromyography (sEMG) typically relies on multichannel sensor arrays and computationally intensive models. This limits practical deployment in low-power and embedded systems. This study investigates the feasibility of classifying ten hand gestures using a single sEMG channel combined with lightweight machine learning architectures. Raw sEMG signals were transformed into a comprehensive feature-based representation, including time-domain, frequency-domain, higher-order-crossing, and relative-intensity features. Feature redundancy was reduced using Pearson correlation filtering and the removal of highly correlated features, while dimensionality-reduction techniques (LDA and PCA) were applied selectively. Three classifiers, a feed-forward neural network (NN), k-nearest neighbors (KNN), and a support vector machine (SVM), were systematically evaluated across four experiments. Results demonstrate that combining time and frequency features with Pearson filtering and a compact NN can achieve up to 90 percent accuracy, even with limited temporal and spatial information. These findings highlight the potential of single-channel sEMG systems for cost-effective, low-power gesture-recognition applications.

</details>


### [57] [DebrisTracer: Reliable Tracking in Hypervelocity Impact Fast Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15986)
*Théophane Loloum,Fabien Vivodtzev,David Hébert,Baptiste Reynier,Michel Arrigoni,Julien Tierny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DebrisTracer 是一个基于拓扑跟踪框架的碎片追踪框架，通过集成领域知识和物理假设，实现对超高速撞击成像中碎片质量和速度分布的可靠估计与可视化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空航天应用中，超高速撞击产生的碎片质量和速度分布的可靠估计至关重要，但现有工具在噪声大、高度特化的数据集上难以实现准确、可靠的碎片追踪。

Method: 基于现成的拓扑跟踪框架（关键点提取与匹配），通过融入领域知识和物理假设进行扩展，自动实现准确的碎片追踪，并提供可解释的可视化分析。

Result: 在多个用例（不同撞击角度和物理条件）上，该方法在物理验证（预测实验喷射质量和弹坑深度剖面）方面显著优于领域专家使用的既有工具；统计摘要能够可视化识别碎片群体中的不同运动形态，印证并细化了专家的先验预期。

Conclusion: DebrisTracer 提供了准确可靠的碎片追踪能力，支持可解释的时空现象可视化分析，且代码与数据库已开源。

Abstract: This application paper presents DebrisTracer, a framework for the reliable tracking of debris in hypervelocity impact fast imaging. These noisy and highly specific datasets capture the ejection of a large number of debris fragments after the impact of a projectile launched at hypervelocity into a target material. The reliable estimation of debris mass and speed distributions is of major importance in aerospace applications. We document how to extend an off-the-shelf topology tracking framework based on critical point extraction and matching, in order to incorporate domain knowledge and physical assumptions. Our approach automatically produces an accurate and reliable debris tracking, enabling an interpretable visual analysis of this complex space-time phenomenon. Extensive experiments demonstrate the accuracy improvements provided by our approach over established tools used by domain experts in terms of physical validation, specifically via the prediction of the experimental ejected mass and crater depth profiles. We illustrate the utility of our approach across several use cases (with varying impact angles and physics). We show that our statistical summaries enable the visual identification of distinct regimes within the debris population, corroborating and refining prior expectations of domain experts. Our database and our C++ implementation are available at this address: https://github.com/tloloum/DebrisTracer.

</details>


### [58] [Presentation, Not Mechanism: A Render Confound in Deprecation-Aware Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16019)
*Zhaoyang Jiang,Zhizhong Fu,Zicheng Li,Yunsoo Kim,Jiacong Mi,Xuanqi Peng,Fei Teng,Honghan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了证据状态修订问题，发现细粒度机制（RevisionLedger）的表面优势（+0.182）几乎全部来自呈现方式的改进，而非机制本身（残差仅+0.021~+0.025）。控制呈现后，粗粒度失效机制反而更优（+0.084），提出记忆评估应固定渲染方式，部署时应使用能覆盖查询的最粗保留状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地从会自我修订的记录（如议题线程、百科历史、政策日志、长对话）中检索信息，面临的关键挑战不仅是找到相关证据，还要判断哪些主张仍然有效、哪些已被取代、何时应弃权。现有结构化记忆方法虽提出带类型边、时间更新和冲突状态的解决方案，但评估往往同时改变机制和提示呈现方式，存在混杂变量。

Method: 在GitHub、多仓库议题历史、Wikipedia和DyKnow风格时间流上构建了2,907个高一致性问题的基准。比较了三种方法：扁平检索、粗粒度边失效、细粒度RevisionLedger。引入了渲染匹配控制（相同布局，禁用弃用标记）以分离呈现方式和机制本身的影响。

Result: 细粒度机制（RevisionLedger）的表面优势（+0.182）几乎全部来自呈现方式改进。控制呈现后，粗粒度失效是唯一对当前状态查询有正向贡献的机制，比细粒度账本高出0.084。查询充分性原则表明溯源主要需要保留已失效的证据，而非更丰富的类型标注。

Conclusion: 记忆评估应固定渲染方式，弃用感知系统应部署能覆盖其查询的最粗保留状态。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly retrieve from records that revise themselves: issue threads, encyclopedic histories, policy logs, and long conversations. The challenge is not only finding relevant evidence, but deciding which claims remain in force, which were superseded, and when to abstain. Structured memories promise to solve this with typed edges, temporal updates, and conflict status, yet evaluations often change mechanism and prompt presentation together. We study this as Evidence-State Revision, comparing flat retrieval, coarse edge invalidation, and fine-grained RevisionLedger on 2,907 high-agreement questions from GitHub, multi-repo issue histories, Wikipedia, and DyKnow-style temporal streams. A render-matched control (same layout, deprecation disabled) reveals the central confound: when a value is changed and later restored, RevisionLedger appears to beat a flat baseline by +0.182, but almost all the gain comes from easier presentation; the fine-grained mechanism residual is indistinguishable from zero (+0.021 to +0.025 across two judge families). After presentation is controlled, coarse invalidation is the only mechanism that pays for current-state queries, beating the fine ledger by 0.084; the same query-sufficiency principle says provenance mainly needs retained invalidated evidence, not richer typing. Memory evaluations should hold render fixed, and deprecation-aware systems should deploy the coarsest retained state that covers their queries.

</details>


### [59] [Constrained Hebbian Learning Supports Efficient Representational Allocation under Structural Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16027)
*Patrick Inoue,Florian Röhrbein,Andreas Knoblauch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了兴奋性竞争Hebbian学习规则在生物代谢和连接性约束下的突触资源分配能力。使用变分信息瓶颈的互信息度量表示成本，在三个音视频基准上比较Hebbian学习与DDTP和BP。结果表明Hebbian学习以更低的任务信息成本（CTI）达到可比的功能性能，支持其作为突触资源分配机制而非通用分类优化策略的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统面临突触维护成本高昂和连接性有限的约束，这需要能够将行为相关信息压缩为低冗余模式的神经编码。本文旨在测试兴奋性竞争Hebbian规则能否在这种约束下支持突触资源分配，以及其产生的表示是否比参考学习规则占据更优的成本-性能权衡区间。

Method: 使用基于变分信息瓶颈的互信息度量量化表示成本；采用三个音视频基准（AVE、Kinetics-Sounds、VGGSound100）的固定音视频嵌入来隔离下游联想可塑性；在匹配稀疏性和架构约束条件下，将Hebbian学习与密集差异目标传播（DDTP）和反向传播（BP）进行比较。

Result: 在主要压缩比较中，Hebbian学习的任务信息成本（CTI）低于稀疏BP和DDTP，与非负权重的浅层BP达到可比的CTI值。Hebbian学习并未统一提升分类性能，而是改变了任务相关信息与表示成本之间的权衡，在多个设置中以更低CTI达到可比的功能性能。

Conclusion: Hebbian学习体现的是成本-性能权衡而非统一的准确率提升。在给定任务相关信息水平下，Hebbian表示保留了更少的输入信息但保持了功能性能，尽管在某些数据集上准确率略有下降。这些发现支持将Hebbian学习解释为突触资源分配机制，而非最大化音视频分类准确率的通用策略。

Abstract: Introduction: Biological systems face anatomical and metabolic constraints, including costly synaptic maintenance and limited connectivity. These constraints favor neural codes that compress behaviorally relevant information into low-redundancy patterns. We test whether an excitatory competitive Hebbian rule can support synaptic resource allocation under such constraints and whether the resulting representations occupy a more favorable cost-performance regime than reference learning rules.
  Methods: Representational cost is quantified using mutual-information-based measures derived from the Variational Information Bottleneck. Experiments use fixed audiovisual embeddings from three audiovisual benchmarks (AVE, Kinetics-Sounds, VGGSound100) to isolate downstream associative plasticity. Hebbian learning is compared with Dense Difference Target Propagation (DDTP) and backpropagation (BP) under matched sparsity and architectural constraints.
  Results: Hebbian learning achieves lower task-information cost (CTI) than sparse BP and DDTP in the main compressed comparisons, while reaching CTI values comparable to shallow BP with nonnegative weights. Rather than uniformly improving classification performance, Hebbian learning shifts the trade-off between task-relevant information and representational cost, yielding lower CTI at comparable functional performance in several settings.
  Discussion: The results indicate a cost-performance trade-off rather than uniform accuracy gains. For a given level of task-relevant information, Hebbian representations retain less input information while preserving functional performance, although accuracy is slightly reduced on some datasets. These findings support interpreting Hebbian learning as a mechanism for synaptic resource allocation rather than as a general strategy for maximizing audiovisual classification accuracy.

</details>


### [60] [CLaC@FinMMEval 2026 Task 3: Sentiment-Augmented Deep Reinforcement Learning for Active Trading -- An Alpha-Reward Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16028)
*Andrei Neagu,Eeham Khan,Leila Kosseim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对CLEF 2026 FinMMEval Lab任务3，将每日交易决策建模为离散动作马尔可夫决策过程，比较四种深度强化学习算法（PG、PPO、DQL、DDPG），使用技术指标、循环日历编码和LLaMA 3.2 1B生成的新闻情感分数。引入基于超额市场回报的Alpha奖励和随机化episode起始日期以减少过拟合。通过Ray Tune进行超参数优化。DDPG在测试集上整体表现最佳；DQL因验证Sharpe比率最高被选用于线上端点。TSLA上DDPG和DQL累计回报达54.96%和52.62%（买入持有为16.45%），BTC上DDPG实现1.58%正回报（买入持有下跌34.27%）。研究揭示了从牛市到熊市策略迁移的泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用深度强化学习处理金融交易决策，目标是超越传统的买入持有策略，并应对新闻情绪与市场数据融合的挑战。

Method: 将每日交易决策形式化为离散动作MDP；比较四种DRL算法（PG、PPO、DQL、DDPG）；使用技术指标、循环日历编码和LLaMA 3.2 1B提取的新闻情感分数作为特征；引入Alpha奖励（超额市场回报）并随机化episode起始日期以缓解过拟合；通过Ray Tune进行180次超参数搜索，基于验证Sharpe比率进行早停和模型选择。

Result: DDPG在CLEF任务3测试集上整体表现最强。TSLA上DDPG和DQL累计回报分别为54.96%和52.62%（买入持有16.45%）。BTC上DDPG正回报1.58%（买入持有-34.27%）。存在显著的验证-测试泛化差距。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习，尤其是DDPG和DQL，在金融交易中能显著超越买入持有基线。但从牛市验证到熊市测试的策略迁移存在泛化挑战，需进一步研究跨市场环境的鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents our system for Task 3 of the CLEF 2026 FinMMEval Lab, which requires daily long, flat, or short trading decisions for Bitcoin (BTC) and Tesla (TSLA) using news and historical market data. We formulate the problem as a discrete-action Markov Decision Process and compare four deep reinforcement learning algorithms: Policy Gradient (PG), Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Deep Q-Learning (DQL), and Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG). The agents use technical indicators, cyclical calendar encodings, and daily news sentiment scores produced by LLaMA 3.2 1B. To reduce overfitting and align training with the objective of outperforming buy-and-hold, we introduce an alpha reward based on excess market return and randomize episode start dates. Hyperparameters are optimized with Ray Tune over 180 trials per algorithm-asset pair, with early stopping and model selection based on validation Sharpe ratio. On the CLEF Task 3 test set, DDPG achieves the strongest overall performance. DQL was selected a priori for the live endpoint because it obtained the highest validation Sharpe ratio, with selection performed without access to the test period. For TSLA, DDPG and DQL achieve cumulative returns of 54.96% and 52.62%, respectively, compared with 16.45% for buy-and-hold. For BTC, DDPG achieves a positive return of 1.58% while buy-and-hold declines by -34.27%. The results also reveal a substantial validation-to-test generalization gap, highlighting the difficulty of transferring policies selected in bull-market conditions to a bear-market regime.

</details>


### [61] [DELUGE: Towards Continental-Scale Daily Pluvial Flood Damage Prediction via Interpretable Conditioning on Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16050)
*Yuya Kawakami,Daniel Cayan,Dongyu Liu,Kwan-Liu Ma,Tom Corringham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DELUGE是一个多模态深度学习框架，用于美国本土每日暴雨洪涝灾害损失预测，分辨率约1公里，通过创新的可解释参数化模块在空间留出验证下比传统机器学习模型（随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM）的PR-AUC提升9%-30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暴雨洪涝占美国NFIP索赔的45%，但现有预测方法要么分辨率粗、局限于区域尺度，要么基于计算密集的物理过程模型，无法满足日常大陆尺度的使用需求。

Method: 提出多模态深度学习框架DELUGE，融合灾害风险三要素（致灾因子、暴露度、脆弱性），创新点在于水文气象分支中的两个参数化模块——Value Modulator和Temporal Modulator，基于地形描述符和AlphaEarth基础模型嵌入进行条件化，提供可解释的水文响应参数和架构层面的可解释性。

Result: 在空间块留出验证下，DELUGE在加权PR-AUC指标上比调优后的随机森林、XGBoost和LightGBM基线提升9%-30%，该指标侧重评估罕见高成本索赔，具有实际运营价值。

Conclusion: DELUGE实现了国家尺度每天约1公里分辨率的暴雨洪涝损失预测，同时其可解释条件化方案可作为将基础模型嵌入集成到其他地理空间预测任务中的可迁移范式。

Abstract: Pluvial (rainfall-driven) flooding accounts for 45% of National Flood Insurance Program (NFIP) claims in the United States and is harder to predict than its riverine and coastal counterparts, with existing approaches limited to coarse resolution, regional domains, or computationally intensive process-based models unsuitable for daily continental-scale use. We present DELUGE, a multimodal deep learning framework for daily pluvial flood damage prediction at ~1 km resolution and national scale, trained on spatially and temporally corrected NFIP claims (2017-2022) and structured around the hazard, exposure, and vulnerability components of disaster risk. Rather than blanket coverage of the Conterminous United States (CONUS), we model the top 100 highest-claim 75 km cells, distributed nationwide and accounting for ~81% of total pluvial flood claims. Our architectural novelty is a pair of parametric modules in the hydrometeorology branch, a Value Modulator and a Temporal Modulator, conditioned on terrain descriptors and AlphaEarth foundation-model embeddings, that expose directly inspectable hydrological response parameters and provide architecture-level interpretability-by-design. Under a spatial block holdout, DELUGE outperforms tuned Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM baselines by 9% to 30% on a dollar-weighted area under the precision-recall curve (PR-AUC), a metric that emphasizes the rare, high-cost claims of greatest operational interest. Beyond DELUGE, we argue this interpretable conditioning scheme is a transferable pattern for integrating foundation-model embeddings into other geospatial prediction tasks.

</details>


### [62] [Physics-Based Deep Spatiotemporal Hyperlocal Radar Nowcasting with a Multi-Variable U-Net for High-Resolution Precipitation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16080)
*Akshay Sunil,Muhammed Rashid,Raja Sekhar Sivaraju,Sushma Nair,Subimal Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用编码器-解码器U-Net架构，输入多仰角反射率、多普勒径向速度和径向速度梯度代理特征（速度幅度、散度类似量、方向切变和涡度类似量通道），无需完整风场反演。加入高反射率注意力模块增强对流核心敏感性，并通过物理引导归因检验学习特征的物理合理性。

Result: 在90分钟预报时，≥10、≥20、≥30 dBZ阈值的CSI分别为0.437、0.332、0.193；相比持续性预报，模型在长预报时效下RMSE更低、空间相关性更高；训练后可在标准计算机上秒级运行。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting over the immediate 10-90 min period is important for flood management and real-time decision-making in urban regions. Conventional short-range forecasting with high-resolution numerical weather prediction requires frequent data assimilation, model initialization, and spin-up, introducing computational latency. Machine learning provides an alternative by learning storm evolution directly from high-frequency observations and producing forecasts quickly after training. This is particularly relevant for Mumbai, India, where monsoon convection, land-sea interactions, and localized intense rainfall make short-term prediction difficult. Here, we develop a compact radar-only nowcasting framework that combines multi-elevation reflectivity, Doppler radial velocity, and radial-velocity-gradient proxy features within an encoder-decoder U-Net. Using the most recent radar volume scan, the model predicts 12 future composite reflectivity fields at 7.5-min intervals up to 90 min lead time. The derived velocity magnitude, divergence-like, directional-shear, and vorticity-like channels represent kinematic signatures associated with convergence and boundary interactions without requiring full wind-field retrieval. A high-reflectivity attention module improves sensitivity to convective cores, and physics-guided attribution examines whether the learned sensitivities are meteorologically meaningful. The model is trained using Mumbai Doppler radar observations from May to August 2023 and evaluated on temporally independent events. At 90 min lead time, Critical Success Index values are 0.437, 0.332, and 0.193 for $\geq$10, $\geq$20, and $\geq$30 dBZ thresholds, respectively. Compared with persistence, the model gives lower RMSE and higher spatial correlation at longer lead times. Once trained, it runs on a standard computer, generating nowcasts within seconds for real-time use.

</details>


### [63] [Neural spectroscopy of AlphaFold2 reveals encoded protein conformational landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16087)
*Kaustav Mehta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaFold2的9300万参数不仅可用于结构预测，更是一个可被直接分析的科学对象——通过高斯卷积平滑并缩放其权重张量，可以揭示出物理上合理的构象景观。该方法"神经光谱学"表明模型编码了超越静态预测的结构约束信息，构象组织作为训练目标的副产物而涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上AlphaFold2的权重仅被视为序列到结构的转换"机器"，本文认为这些权重本身编码了蛋白质构象组织的知识，可直接作为科学对象进行分析，而非仅作为预测工具使用。

Method: 采用缩放高斯卷积（Scaled Gaussian Convolution）对Evoformer权重张量进行平滑和缩放处理，扰动模型以观察构象景观变化；并通过匹配功率的噪声控制实验作为对照。

Result: (1) 泛素扰动下天然接触断裂顺序与数十年实验结果一致；(2) KaiB的五个独立训练模型在扰动下均未恢复替代折叠；(3) α-突触核蛋白的五个模型产生各异但连贯的景观；(4) 噪声控制产生碎片而非构象。

Conclusion: AlphaFold2的权重编码了超越静态预测的结构约束信息，构象组织作为副产物从结构预测目标中涌现。提出的"神经光谱学"方法可用于直接读取模型编码的蛋白质构象知识。

Abstract: AlphaFold2's 93 million parameters, shaped by the evolutionary record of protein structure encoded in the Protein Data Bank and in sequence alignments, are conventionally treated only as machinery for converting sequence to structure. We propose they are also a scientific object that can be analyzed directly: a learned encoding of protein conformational organization that can be probed and characterized. By smoothing the Evoformer's weight tensors with a Gaussian convolution and scaling the result, we show that the trained model produces physically structured conformational landscapes. Under perturbation, ubiquitin's native contacts break in the order established by decades of folding experiments. For KaiB, five independently trained models agree that the alternative fold is not recovered under perturbation. For alpha-synuclein, five models produce five different but coherent landscapes, mapping where the training signal has determined the representation and where it has not. Matched-power noise controls confirm that random corruption of equal magnitude produces debris, not conformations. The model learned to predict static structures; the conformational organization visible under perturbation was not an explicit training target, suggesting it emerged as a byproduct of that objective. AlphaFold2's weights appear to encode structural constraints, shaped by evolutionary and structural training data, that extend beyond what unperturbed inference reveals. We call the approach of reading them neural spectroscopy, and Scaled Gaussian Convolution one such protocol.

</details>


### [64] [The Honest Quorum Problem: Epistemic Byzantine Fault Tolerance for Agentic Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16109)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出**认知拜占庭容错（EBFT）**模型，针对**智能体验证者（agentic validators）**可能因推理错误而背书语义无效状态转换的问题。EBFT在传统拜占庭故障界之外，引入两个独立、置信度索引的参数：$e_δ$（拜占庭集外的一致性无效背书数量）和$u_ε$（退化活跃性的不可用验证者支持），并推导了语义有效性、共识协议、活跃性及阈值选择的仲裁条件，以及预算估计的校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SMR和BFT共识假设协议合规即语义正确，但**智能体验证者**即使协议合规也可能因推理错误（认知故障）背书无效转换。此外，智能体间因共享模型权重、训练数据、提示或工具链而存在**相关认知故障**风险。现有模型无法刻画此类故障模式，需新的容错模型。

Method: 定义**认知拜占庭容错（EBFT）**模型，在传统拜占庭故障界之外引入两个置信度索引参数$e_δ$（拜占庭集外一致性无效背书）和$u_ε$（不可用支持）。推导了语义有效性、共识协议、活跃性的仲裁阈值条件，并给出阈值选择的可行性条件及预算校准方法论。

Result: 证明了仅增加名义上不同的智能体并不能提升容错性，除非能**可测量地降低无效背书或不可用支持的上尾集中度**。EBFT模型为智能体基础设施和后确定性分布式系统提供了独立量化语义安全风险与活跃性退化的形式化框架。

Conclusion: 共识协议仅保证协议合规，不保证语义有效性。EBFT为智能体验证者场景提供了更精细的容错模型，区分了语义安全风险与活跃性退化，并指出了提升容错性的有效途径是降低认知故障的集中度而非简单增加节点数。

Abstract: State machine replication (SMR) and Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus guarantee agreement despite a bounded number of arbitrary, colluding faulty participants. However, these guarantees rely on participants outside this set correctly executing the protocol's transition semantics. Agentic validators expose a weaker boundary: an authenticated, responsive, non-equivocating, and protocol-compliant reasoning participant may still endorse a semantically invalid transition due to reasoning errors.
  We call this failure mode an epistemic fault, and the collective phenomenon the Honest Quorum Problem (where "honest" means protocol-compliant, not semantically correct). Such a quorum can satisfy ordinary checks while forming a certificate for an invalid transition. Thus, agreement alone does not guarantee semantic validity or execution safety. Furthermore, because agentic validators often share model weights, training distributions, prompts, or toolchains, they are highly susceptible to correlated epistemic faults.
  We define Epistemic Byzantine Fault Tolerance (EBFT), a fault-tolerance model for agentic infrastructure and post-deterministic distributed systems. EBFT augments the conventional Byzantine fault bound with two separate, confidence-indexed quantities: $e_δ$ bounds coherent invalid endorsements outside the Byzantine set, and $u_ε$ bounds unusable validator support that degrades liveness. These quantities characterize semantic safety risk and liveness degradation independently. We derive quorum-threshold conditions for semantic validity, consensus agreement, liveness, and feasible threshold selection, and outline a calibration methodology for estimating these budgets. We show that adding nominally distinct agents improves fault tolerance only when it measurably reduces the upper-tail concentration of invalid endorsements or unusable support.

</details>


### [65] [Improving Improved Kernel PLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16138)
*Ole-Christian Galbo Engstrøm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对IKPLS算法中的两个关键步骤（X旋转矩阵R和Y载荷矩阵Q的计算）提出加速改进：对R采用直接评估策略以提升并行性，对Q首次推导出等价关系将计算复杂度从Θ(KM)降至Θ(M)，在CPU和GPU上分别实现约2倍和6倍的整体加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IKPLS算法是高效的PLS校准算法，但其中R和Q的计算仍有优化空间，特别是Q的计算复杂度较高，且现有改进未充分利用现代硬件的并行能力。

Method: 1) 对R：将逐项累加替换为直接评估策略，保持相同乘法次数但更好并行化；2) 对Q：首次发现每个Y载荷可从同一迭代中已计算的量通过显式推导的常数获得，将复杂度从Θ(KM)降至Θ(M)（当M=1或2≤M<K时）。改进后的算法与原始算法产生完全相同的W, P, Q, R, T。

Result: 在NumPy (CPU)和JAX (GPU)上，孤立步骤加速达两个数量级；整体拟合在CPU上约2倍加速，GPU上约6倍加速。所有改进已在开源Python包ikpls中实现。

Conclusion: 提出的加速方法在不改变算法输出的前提下显著提升了IKPLS的计算效率，且对M较小（M<K）的场景尤其有效，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Improved Kernel Partial Least Squares (IKPLS) algorithms 1 and 2 are among the fastest PLS calibration algorithms. This article focuses on two shared steps, the computation of the $\mathbf{X}$ rotations, $\mathbf{R}$, and the $\mathbf{Y}$ loadings, $\mathbf{Q}$, and accelerates both. For $\mathbf{R}$, term-by-term accumulation is replaced by a direct evaluation strategy that requires the same number of multiplications but parallelizes better on modern hardware. For $\mathbf{Q}$, I identify - to the best of my knowledge, for the first time - equivalences showing that each $\mathbf{Y}$ loading is obtainable, up to explicitly derived constants, from quantities already computed earlier in the same iteration, and I exploit them in IKPLS to reduce the cost of each loading from $Θ\left(KM\right)$ to $Θ\left(M\right)$ operations whenever $M = 1$ or $2 \leq M < K$, with $K$ predictor variables (number of columns in $\mathbf{X}$) and $M$ response variables (number of columns in $\mathbf{Y}$). Both improvements provably yield exactly the same $\mathbf{W}$, $\mathbf{P}$, $\mathbf{Q}$, $\mathbf{R}$, and $\mathbf{T}$ as the original algorithms. Benchmarks with NumPy (CPU) and JAX (GPU) show speedups of up to two orders of magnitude for the isolated steps and of approximately $2\times$ (CPU) and $6\times$ (GPU) for entire fits. Both improvements are implemented in the free, open-source Python package \texttt{ikpls}.

</details>


### [66] [PRISA: Proactive Infrastructure LiDAR Framework for Intersection Safety Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16156)
*Tam Bang,Hussam Abubakr,Emiliano de la Garza Villarreal,Truc Phuong Nguyen,Austin Harris,Toru Hirano,Mina Sartipi,Yunfei Xu,Hoang H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISA是一个模块化基础设施LiDAR框架，利用隐私保护、低光鲁棒的 roadside 传感器实现长期交通观测和边缘实时风险检测。该框架包含感知层和即插即用风险评估模块，可自动从累积感知输出中整理站点特定的训练数据以训练轨迹预测模型，无需人工标注。在 R-LiViT 数据集和 Tennessee 州 Chattanooga 市实际信号交叉口部署评估，PPET 评估在2.4秒预测时域内实现194ms端到端延迟，满足实时性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交叉路口是道路网络中最危险的地点之一，对车辆和弱势道路使用者（如行人、骑行者）构成重大风险。多智能体交互的复杂性需要持续的实时监控系统，能够在冲突升级为碰撞之前进行预测。

Method: PRISA包含两个核心组件：（1）感知层，利用隐私保护、低光鲁棒的 LiDAR 传感器；（2）即插即用风险评估模块，自动从感知输出整理站点特定训练数据训练轨迹预测模型（无需人工标注），部署后持续进行运动预测和双重替代安全评估——纵向冲突使用 TTC（碰撞时间），交叉及VRU交互使用 PPET（预测后侵占时间）。

Result: 在 R-LiViT 数据集和 Tennessee 州 Chattanooga 市实际信号交叉口（部署于 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor）评估。PPET 评估在2.4秒预测时域内实现194ms端到端延迟，TTC 检测和感知均满足实时约束。

Conclusion: PRISA 展示了面向多智能体交叉路口主动安全监控的实用可行性。

Abstract: Urban intersections are among the most hazardous locations in road networks, posing significant risks to vehicles and vulnerable road users (VRUs) such as pedestrians and cyclists. The complexity of multi-agent interactions demands continuous, real-time monitoring systems capable of anticipating conflicts before they escalate into crashes. We present PRISA, a modular infrastructure LiDAR framework leveraging privacy-preserving, low-light-robust roadside sensors for long-term traffic observation and real-time risk detection at the edge. The framework comprises two core components: a sensing and perception layer and a plug-and-play risk assessment module. The latter automatically curates site-specific training data from accumulated perception outputs to train a trajectory prediction model without manual annotation. It then deploys the trained model for continuous motion forecasting and dual surrogate safety evaluation, using Time-to-Collision (TTC) for longitudinal conflicts and Predicted Post-Encroachment Time (PPET) for crossing and VRU-involved interactions. PRISA is evaluated on the public R-LiViT dataset and deployed on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor at a live signalized intersection in Chattanooga, Tennessee. PPET-based assessment operates at 194~ms end-to-end latency over a 2.4-second predictive horizon, with TTC-based detection and perception remaining within real-time constraints, demonstrating practical feasibility for proactive multi-agent intersection safety monitoring.

</details>


### [67] [Behaviour-Conditioned Neural Processes for Adaptive Residential Short-Term Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16168)
*Ramin Soleimani,Andrea Visentin,Dirk Pesch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出行为条件化注意力神经过程（BC-ANP）框架，将推断出的用户行为结构嵌入概率预测模型中进行短时负荷预测，在SGSC数据集上相比基线方法，MAE降低7.9%，CRPS降低6.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅短时负荷预测因用户需求异质性强、时间变化大且受多样化行为模式影响而极具挑战。现有方法通常将行为结构仅用作外部分组信号，未嵌入预测模型内部，本文旨在探索能否将推断的行为结构嵌入神经过程概率模型的预测机制中。

Method: 提出行为条件化注意力神经过程（BC-ANP）框架：1）将每个负荷曲线视为一个预测任务；2）用离散潜变量表示行为结构，从可用上下文中推断并用于解码器条件化；3）连续潜变量捕捉异质曲线间的共享功能不确定性；4）训练时使用聚类信息提供弱监督，测试时仅依赖上下文推断的类别分布。

Result: 在SGSC数据集上，使用用户不重叠的训练/验证/测试划分、可变上下文长度和多步预测。BC-ANP变体在所有预测步长和上下文设置下均优于ANP基线，MAE和CRPS分别平均降低7.9%和6.9%；在有限上下文中增益最大；与固定窗口基线相比，在所有评估步长下RMSE更低且MAE具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将行为结构嵌入神经过程模型的预测机制是有效的，支持跨异质用户、上下文和预测步长的单模型、不确定性感知负荷预测。

Abstract: Residential short-term load forecasting (STLF) is challenging because household demand is heterogeneous, temporally variable, and shaped by diverse behavioural routines. This work investigates whether inferred behavioural structure can be embedded within the forecasting mechanism of a Neural Process-based probabilistic model, rather than used only as an external grouping signal, for context-conditioned residential STLF. We propose a behaviour-conditioned Attentive Neural Process framework that treats each load profile as a forecasting task. Behavioural structure is represented by a discrete latent variable inferred from the available context and used for behaviour-conditioned decoder conditioning, while a continuous latent variable captures shared functional uncertainty across heterogeneous profiles. To enable conditioning without ground-truth behavioural labels, clustering-derived information provides weak supervision during training, whereas test-time conditioning relies only on context-inferred class distributions. Experiments on the Smart Grid, Smart City (SGSC) dataset use user-disjoint train/validation/test splits, variable context lengths, and multi-step forecast horizons, with comparisons against a label-agnostic ANP baseline and fixed-window deterministic STLF baselines. The proposed variants improve MAE and CRPS over ANP across horizons and context settings, with the largest gains under limited context. The best-performing variant achieves average reductions of 7.9% in MAE and 6.9% in CRPS relative to ANP. Compared with fixed-window baselines, this variant achieves lower RMSE across all evaluated horizons while maintaining competitive MAE, suggesting fewer large prediction deviations under heterogeneous consumption patterns. These results support single-model, uncertainty-aware forecasting across heterogeneous households, contexts, and horizons.

</details>


### [68] [Physics-enhanced reinforcement learning for real-time optimal control of dynamical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16177)
*Matteo Tomasetto,Nicolò Botteghi,Gabriele Bruni,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理增强强化学习（PEARL）范式，通过actor-adjoint算法利用自动微分和伴随灵敏度计算策略梯度，显著减少环境交互次数，实现对高维参数化动力系统的高效控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL算法在高维状态和动作空间中样本效率低，需要大量环境交互，难以直接应用于高维参数化动力系统的控制问题。

Method: 采用actor-adjoint算法：短时域上利用自动微分计算策略梯度，长时域上通过神经网络近似伴随灵敏度来计算未来回报，从而减少环境交互并缓解长期梯度不稳定性。

Result: 在非定常流中的两个参数化导航问题上，PEARL优于现有RL算法，样本效率高，能够泛化到多种场景，且可扩展到高维状态和动作空间。

Conclusion: PEARL通过融合物理知识与RL，有效解决了高维参数化动力系统控制中的样本效率与可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has recently emerged as a promising feedback control strategy for nonlinear and complex dynamical systems. However, RL algorithms are sample inefficient and require a large number of interaction with the environment to synthesize optimal control strategies. Consequently, applications of RL are typically limited to sparse sensors and actuators due to the curse of dimensionality entailed by the exploration-exploitation dilemma in high-dimensional spaces. In this work, we bridge RL and traditional optimal control for dynamical system with a novel Physics-EnhAnced Reinforcement Learning (PEARL) paradigm tailored to the control of high-dimensional and parametric dynamical systems, exploiting the differentibility of their dynamics. Specifically, PEARL employs an actor-adjoint algorithm that leverages automatic differentiation to compute policy gradients over short horizons and adjoint-based sensitivities of future returns approximated via neural networks, significantly reducing the number of environment interactions, while mitigating long-term gradient instabilities. Through two challenging parametric navigation problems in unsteady flows, we show that PEARL (i) effectively exploits differentiable environments to outperform state-of-the-art RL algorithms, (ii) is sample efficient, thanks to the physics-guided policy learning, (iii) generalizes across multiple scenarios, which is crucial when dealing with parametric systems, and (iv) enables scaling RL to high-dimensional state and action spaces, without requiring low-dimensional state representations or multi-agent strategies.

</details>


### [69] [A Blueprint for Equilibrium-Based Differentiable Continuous-Variable Thermodynamic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16183)
*Owen Lockwood,Jérémy Béjanin,Joost Bus,Christopher Chamberland,Patrick Huembeli,Frank Schäfer,Guillaume Verdon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于热力学计算的硬件蓝图，利用随机模拟电路（如超导电路）中的朗之万动力学来实现能量基模型，并展示了如何构建和训练概率机器学习模型，从理论和数值上分析了其能耗和延迟优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习工作负载对能耗和延迟的需求不断增长，传统数字计算硬件面临瓶颈，亟需探索全新的物理计算范式以实现更高效的计算。

Method: 基于热力学计算框架，利用物理硬件中的随机模拟过程（朗之万动力学）实现可调能量势，构建参数化能量基模型；通过概率图模型框架构建和训练机器学习模型；使用随机模拟超导电路作为初步实验实现。

Result: 从理论和数值上分析了不同模型在热力学范式下的运行时间和能耗；展示了基于热噪声驱动的超导随机模拟电路的初步实验实现。

Conclusion: 这些结果为开发用于概率机器学习的节能热力学硬件指明了路径。

Abstract: To address the escalating energy and latency demands of machine-learning workloads, we introduce a blueprint for an energy-efficient and fast thermodynamic computing stack that leverages stochastic analog processes in physical hardware. In this work, we focus on energy-based thermodynamic computing where the stochastic process is well described by Langevin dynamics with tunable energy potentials. The implementation of such potentials in physical hardware enables us to generate and sample from basic parameterized energy-based models. We demonstrate how to construct and train popular classes of machine learning models based on these hardware-native energy-based models, using the framework of probabilistic graphical models. We analyze the runtime and energy consumption of different models in this thermodynamic paradigm based on theoretical considerations and numerical studies. As a preliminary experimental realization of such hardware, we present our stochastic analog superconducting circuits driven by thermal noise. Together, these results outline a path toward energy-efficient thermodynamic hardware for probabilistic machine learning.

</details>


### [70] [PagedWeight: Efficient MoE LLM Serving with Dynamic Quality-Aware Weight Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16184)
*Yuchen Yang,Yifan Zhao,Anisha Dasgupta,Sasa Misailovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PagedWeight是一种针对MoE大语言模型的新型运行时管理方法，通过动态量化专家权重并在KV缓存与权重精度之间取得平衡，在内存敏感场景下实现高达72%的GPU内存节省、1.94倍吞吐量提升，以及在相似内存预算下质量提升达39.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE大语言模型在KV缓存密集型推理场景中，模型权重的GPU内存需求与不断增长的KV缓存之间存在资源竞争，现有方法未能有效平衡这一矛盾。

Method: 提出PagedWeight方法，在运行时动态量化MoE模型权重，通过精细管理专家权重的精度与KV缓存大小之间的权衡，优化模型的任务准确率、内存消耗和吞吐量/延迟。

Result: 在多个内存敏感的MoE服务场景中，相比已有量化基线，PagedWeight在保持FP16等效精度的同时节省高达72.0%的GPU内存，提升1.94倍吞吐量；在相似内存预算下质量提升达39.3%，吞吐量损失不超过4.1%。

Conclusion: PagedWeight有效解决了MoE LLM服务中权重与KV缓存之间的内存竞争问题，在质量-内存权衡上显著优于现有量化方法。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) is a popular class of large language models (LLMs), offering high efficiency and accuracy. However, in KV-cache-intensive serving scenarios, MoEs often exhibit a tension between the GPU memory requirements of the model weights and the growing KV cache. We propose PagedWeight, a novel management method for MoE LLM serving that dynamically quantizes MoE model's weights at runtime and balances expert-weight precision with the KV cache sizes. PagedWeight exposes and effectively navigates the complex tradeoff between the model's task accuracy, memory consumption, and throughput/latency. Across several memory-sensitive MoE serving scenarios, PagedWeight improves the quality-memory tradeoff over several existing quantization baselines. PagedWeight achieves FP16-equivalent accuracy with up to 72.0% GPU memory savings and 1.94$\times$ throughput improvement, and improves quality over quantization methods by up to 39.3% at a similar memory budget with at most 4.1% throughput loss.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

### [71] [Adaptive Retrieval Strategies for Biomedical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15283)
*Han Yue,Eric Zhou,Alex Hu,Xueshen Li,Yuan Zhang,Jinfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应检索框架，根据问题类型（是非、事实型、列表型、摘要型）动态选择检索和证据聚合策略，在BioASQ基准上验证了该方法能提升证据相关性和答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强的QA系统对不同类型问题（是非/事实/列表/摘要）采用统一的检索管道，忽略了各类问题在证据形式和推理需求上的差异，限制了证据获取和答案生成的效果。

Method: 提出自适应检索框架，融合查询理解、生物医学文档检索、重排序、知识图谱增强、文档聚类和大语言模型答案生成。对是非问题聚焦精确证据检索；对事实型和列表问题强调实体导向检索与聚类；对摘要问题进行更广泛的证据收集与综合。

Result: 在BioASQ基准上评估，自适应检索策略在多种问题类型上均提升了证据相关性和答案质量。

Conclusion: 将检索机制与问题类型特定的信息需求对齐，是增强检索增强型生物医学QA系统的有效方向。

Abstract: Biomedical question answering (QA) encompasses diverse question types, including yes/no, factoid, list, and summary questions, each requiring distinct forms of evidence and reasoning. However, most retrieval-augmented QA systems rely on a unified retrieval pipeline, regardless of the information needs of different question categories. This one-size-fits-all approach may limit the effectiveness of evidence acquisition and downstream answer generation. In this work, we propose an adaptive retrieval framework that selects retrieval and evidence aggregation strategies according to question type. The system combines query understanding, biomedical document retrieval, reranking, knowledge graph augmentation, document clustering, and large language model-based answer generation. For yes/no questions, it focuses on precise evidence retrieval; for factoid and list questions, it emphasizes entity-oriented retrieval and clustering; and for summary questions, it performs broader evidence collection and synthesis. We evaluate the proposed framework on the BioASQ benchmark and demonstrate that adaptive retrieval strategies improve evidence relevance and answer quality across multiple question types. Our results suggest that aligning retrieval mechanisms with question-specific information needs provides an effective direction for enhancing retrieval-augmented biomedical QA systems.

</details>


### [72] [How Does Empowering Users with Greater System Control Affect News Filter Bubbles?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15284)
*Ping Liu,Karthik Shivaram,Aron Culotta,Matthew Shapiro,Mustafa Bilgic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文设计了一个增强界面的政治新闻推荐系统，通过透明化系统推断的用户政治和主题兴趣，让用户能主动调整推荐内容。用户研究发现，该透明方法帮助用户意识到过滤气泡的存在，但对新闻消费的影响因用户偏好而异：多数用户的新闻偏向趋于温和，但部分用户反而走向极端；同时政治多样性有所降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统易导致"过滤气泡"问题，使用户被限制在强化既有观点的信息环境中。用户往往意识不到自己处于过滤气泡中，即使意识到也缺乏直接控制手段。现有研究缺乏让用户可见且可控的推荐系统设计方案。

Method: 设计了一个增强界面的政治新闻推荐系统，透明显示系统从用户行为中推断出的政治立场和主题兴趣，用户可通过界面调整推荐参数。通过用户实验对比该增强系统与传统推荐界面，分析用户对过滤气泡的感知及阅读行为变化。

Result: 透明化界面帮助用户意识到自己处于过滤气泡中；多数用户阅读的新闻趋于温和，但部分用户反而走向更极端的方向；许多用户从极端立场调整到中间立场，但代价是文章的政治多样性降低。

Conclusion: 增强透明性和可控性的推荐系统能提高用户对过滤气泡的感知，但其对新闻消费的影响具有异质性，取决于用户个人偏好；这种设计并不能保证必然增加政治多样性。

Abstract: While recommendation systems enable users to find articles of interest, they can also create ``filter bubbles'' by presenting content that reinforces users' pre-existing beliefs. Users are often unaware that the system placed them in a filter bubble and, even when aware, they often lack direct control over it. To address these issues, we first design a political news recommendation system augmented with an enhanced interface that exposes the political and topical interests the system inferred from user behavior. This allows the user to adjust the recommendation system to receive more articles on a particular topic or presenting a particular political stance. We then conduct a user study to compare our system to a traditional interface and found that the transparent approach helped users realize that they were in a filter bubble. Additionally, the enhanced system led to less extreme news for most users but also allowed others to move the system to more extremes. Similarly, while many users moved the system from extreme liberal/conservative to the center, this came at the expense of reducing political diversity of the articles shown. These findings suggest that, while the proposed system increased awareness of the filter bubbles, it had heterogeneous effects on news consumption depending on user preferences.

</details>


### [73] [LLMs Encode Relevance as a Layer-Wise Cross-Lingual Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15555)
*Pietro Bernardelle,Samaneh Mohtadi,Stefano Civelli,Joel Mackenzie,Gianluca Demartini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究指令微调LLM中查询-文档相关性是否可从残差流激活中线性解码，以及该信号与生成式相关性判断的比较和跨语言迁移能力。实验表明，相关性编码具有深度依赖性，中后层探针性能最强，且验证集选择的探针在多个模型中匹配或优于生成式判断，能更好地保持系统排序。跨语言实验显示部分可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用于IR相关性评估的研究多集中于输出层面（生成的标签或分数），缺乏对模型内部如何表示相关性的理解。本文旨在从表示层面探究LLM中相关性编码的机制。

Method: 使用TREC DL20和MIRACL数据集，以UMBRELA风格提示词驱动中等规模指令微调LLM（4-9B参数），提取每层Transformer最后一token的残差流激活，训练线性探针预测相关性标签，并与生成式判断及人类标注进行对比。

Result: 相关性信号具有深度依赖性：浅层探针性能弱，中后层最强；多个模型中探针匹配或优于生成式判断，且更优地保持系统排序；跨语言迁移虽存在但弱于同语言解码。

Conclusion: LLM内部相关性表示与外部生成表达存在分离；分层探针可用于诊断相关性在模型中的涌现位置、生成式判断何时未能反映内部证据，以及相关性表示在不同语言、数据集和模型家族间的变化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in information retrieval (IR) pipelines as relevance judges and re-rankers. Yet most analyses remain output-centric, evaluating generated labels or scores while offering limited insight into how relevance is represented inside the model. In this work, we study whether query-document (q-d) relevance is linearly decodable from residual-stream activations in instruction-tuned LLMs, how this signal compares with generated relevance judgments, and whether it transfers across languages. Using the TREC DL20 and MIRACL evaluation collections, we guide medium-scale LLMs (4-9B parameters) with UMBRELA-style relevance judgment prompts, extract last-token activations from every transformer layer, and train linear probes to predict relevance labels. We compare probe predictions with generated judgments and use TREC DL20 to test whether probe-derived pseudo-labels preserve system rankings against human judgments. Our results suggest that q-d relevance is encoded as a depth-dependent signal: probe performance is weak in early layers and strongest in middle-to-late layers, indicating that relevance becomes more linearly accessible after contextual integration. Most importantly, in several models, validation-selected probes match or outperform generated judgments and better preserve system rankings, revealing a separation between internal relevance representation and external expression. Multilingual experiments suggest partial cross-language portability, although transfer remains weaker than within-language decoding. Overall, this work provides a representation-level perspective on LLM-based relevance assessment. Layer-wise probing can help diagnose where relevance emerges, when generated judgments fail to reflect internally available evidence, and how relevance representations vary across languages, datasets, and model families.

</details>


### [74] [RecGPT-V3 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15591)
*Bowen Zheng,Chao Yi,Dian Chen,Gaoyang Guo,Han Zhu,Jiakai Tang,Jian Wu,Mao Zhang,Wen Chen,Yifan Lu,Yujie Luo,Yuning Jiang,Zhujin Gao,Bo Zheng,Dixuan Wang,Hao Fang,Jiancai Liu,Jing Yu,Ke Chen,Kewei Zhu,Mingke Xu,Wenjun Yang,Xunke Xi,Zile Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RecGPT-V3 是一个状态化、混合模态的推荐系统，通过记忆中枢、混合模态基础模型和潜在意图推理三大创新，在淘宝"猜你喜欢"流中实现商业指标提升（GMV +3.97%）的同时将服务资源消耗降低 52.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RecGPT-V1/V2 在生产部署中暴露出三个关键挑战：(1) 无状态行为建模导致每次请求重复处理完整用户历史，浪费计算资源；(2) 标签-物品信息瓶颈，自然语言标签在用户理解与物品定位之间构成有损通道；(3) 显式推理效率低下，长链思维导致延迟和计算开销难以承受。

Method: (1) 记忆中枢：维护结构化、持续演化的用户记忆，将长期行为蒸馏为紧凑单元，减少55.8%的用户建模计算；(2) 混合模态基础模型：让LLM联合推理文本标签和语义ID（SID），打开通向物品空间的高带宽通道；(3) 潜在意图推理：将冗长的推理过程内化为紧凑的可学习潜在 token，同时保持可解码为可读解释，输出 token 成本降低 200 倍。

Result: 在淘宝"猜你喜欢"信息流的大规模在线A/B测试中，IPV +1.28%、CTR +1.00%、TC +1.97%、GMV +3.97%，端到端服务资源消耗降低52.4%。

Conclusion: RecGPT-V3 通过状态化建模、混合模态推理和潜在意图内化，有效解决了前代系统在效率、瓶颈和延迟方面的核心问题，证明了该范式在大规模工业推荐系统中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are transforming recommender systems from matching co-occurrence patterns in historical behavior toward reasoning about the intent that drives it. RecGPT-V1 pioneered this paradigm on Taobao by centering user understanding, and RecGPT-V2 scaled it via coordinated multi-agent reasoning; both are deployed in production with consistent gains in user experience and commercial outcomes. However, operating RecGPT at scale reveals three challenges: (1) stateless behavior modeling, where each request reprocesses full user history, wasting computation and discarding prior analysis; (2) a tag-to-item information bottleneck, where natural-language tags form a lossy channel between user understanding and item grounding; and (3) inefficient explicit reasoning, whose lengthy chain-of-thought incurs untenable latency and compute overhead.
  We present RecGPT-V3, a stateful, hybrid-modal recommender that reasons over natural language for open-world knowledge and Semantic IDs (SIDs) for concrete item grounding. A Memory Hub maintains structured, continually evolving user memory that distills long-horizon behavior into condensed units, cutting user-modeling computation by 55.8%. A Hybrid-modal Foundation Model allows the LLM jointly reason over text tags and SIDs, opening a high-bandwidth channel into the item space. Latent Intent Reasoning internalizes verbose rationales into compact learnable latent tokens that remain decodable into readable explanations, lowering output token cost by 200x. Deployed in Taobao's "Guess What You Like" feed, RecGPT-V3 achieves consistent gains in large-scale online A/B tests: IPV +1.28%, CTR +1.00%, TC +1.97%, GMV +3.97%, while cutting end-to-end serving resource consumption by 52.4%.

</details>


### [75] [PCTD: Preference-Guided Counterfactual Task Decomposition for Agent Tool Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15696)
*Chu Zhao,Lei Tang,Minghang Li,Jianzhe Zhao,Guibing Guo,Zhengzong Chen,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对任务分解中强化学习方法利用检索指标作为奖励导致的奖励黑客问题（如重复分解），提出PCTD框架，通过反事实奖励切断分解与检索之间的虚假相关性，并引入偏好奖励进行细粒度结构监督，在移动多轮交互基准MTDTool上超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的任务分解方法直接采用工具检索指标（Recall/NDCG）作为奖励，导致模型通过重复分解等策略最大化检索匹配，形成分解浅层特征与检索指标之间的虚假相关性，损害了在未见工具上的OOD泛化能力。

Method: 提出PCTD框架：(1) 反事实奖励：量化分解对检索排名的边际因果增益，从源头切断虚假相关；(2) 偏好奖励：对逻辑连贯性和原子性进行细粒度结构监督；(3) 构建MTDTool基准：面向移动多轮交互的任务分解数据集。

Result: 实验表明PCTD能有效缓解重复分解问题，在检索性能、分解质量和OOD泛化方面均超越现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 通过反因果视角解决任务分解中的奖励黑客问题，证明了切断虚假相关性对于提升OOD泛化能力的重要性。

Abstract: Task decomposition aims to transform ambiguous instructions into executable atomic subtasks, thereby guiding high-precision tool retrieval. However, our analysis reveals that directly adopting tool retrieval metrics, i.e., Recall or NDCG, as rewards for task decomposition can easily induce reward hacking in reinforcement learning-based methods. Specifically, models tend to maximize retrieval matching through strategies such as repetitive decomposition. This spurious correlation between the shallow features of decomposition results and retrieval metric impairs generalization in Out-of-Domain (OOD) scenarios involving unseen tools. To address this issue, we propose PCTD, a Preference-guided Counterfactual Task Decomposition framework. PCTD quantifies the marginal causal gain of decomposition on retrieval ranking through a counterfactual reward, thereby cutting off spurious correlations at their source. Meanwhile, it introduces a preference reward to impose fine-grained structural supervision on logical coherence and atomicity, encouraging the model to generate high-quality decompositions. In addition, we construct MTDTool, the task decomposition benchmark specifically designed for mobile multi-turn interactions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PCTD alleviates repetitive decomposition and surpasses SOTA methods in retrieval, decomposition quality, and OOD generalization.

</details>


### [76] [From Skill Extraction to Multistakeholder Recommendation: A Two-Stage Framework for Bias Governance in Skills-Based Job Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15707)
*Andrea Forster,Gregor Autischer,Dominik Kowald,Simone Kopeinik*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段框架，用于检测和治理基于技能的职位匹配中的偏见：第一阶段处理技能提取和档案形成中的偏见风险，第二阶段通过多利益相关者推荐系统和社会选择聚合实现可审计的公平推荐，并采用硬约束/软约束区分及AI Act对齐的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI的劳动力市场系统可能在候选人排名或招聘决策之前就已影响就业机会获取，技能提取、档案形成和候选人-职位匹配中的偏见可能导致对候选人的不公平对待，因此需要系统性的偏见检测与治理框架。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：Stage 1（技能提取与档案形成）关注基于聊天机器人的信息 elicitation 及其偏见风险；Stage 2（多利益相关者候选人-职位推荐）将信息嵌入推荐系统，由候选人、公司和监管机构三个智能体分别生成独立排序，通过社会选择聚合生成可审计的推荐。采用硬约束（需即时修正）与软约束（记录日志供后续决策）的区分。基于AI Act对齐的Fraunhofer AI评估目录，使用分布审计和反事实测试生成Stage 1偏见清单，软约束用于设定Stage 2公平阈值。

Result: Stage 1的偏见清单按硬/软约束分类，软约束为Stage 2的公平阈值提供依据；Stage 2中，超过预设阈值的公平指标会触发适应性推荐流程，较小偏差则记录为偏见报告和持续公平状态。

Conclusion: 该框架通过两阶段设计、多利益相关者推荐与社会选择聚合，结合硬/软约束区分和AI Act对齐的评估方法，实现了对技能匹配中偏见的系统检测与可审计治理。

Abstract: AI-based labor-market systems or platforms can affect access to job opportunities prior to organizational candidate rankings or hiring decisions. Such applications warrant caution, as biases in skill extraction, profile formation, and candidate-job matching may contribute to unfair treatment of candidates. In this paper, we propose a two-stage framework for detecting and governing bias in skills-based job matching. Stage 1, skill extraction and profile formation, addresses how candidates provide skills and preferences to the system, how the system extracts and structures this information, and the bias risks this entails, with a focus on chatbot-based elicitation. Stage 2, multistakeholder candidate-job recommendation, would embed this information in a recommender system in which candidate, company, and regulatory objectives are represented by separate agents, each producing an independent candidate-job ranking; these rankings would be combined through social choice-based aggregation into a single, auditable recommendation. The two stages are connected by a shared distinction between hard constraints, which require correction before processing continues, and soft constraints, which are logged to inform later decisions. Following an AI Act-aligned assessment methodology (based on the Fraunhofer AI Assessment Catalog), we propose using distributional auditing and counterfactual testing to produce a Stage 1 bias inventory sorted into hard and soft constraints, with the latter informing fairness thresholds for Stage 2. The same logic would apply to Stage 2: fairness metrics crossing predefined thresholds would trigger an adapted recommendation process, while smaller deviations would be logged as bias reports and persistent fairness states.

</details>


### [77] [RECAP: Feedback-Driven Streaming Semantic User Profiles for Short-Video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15730)
*Ziyi Zhao,Xiaoyou Zhou,Xiao Lv,Yangyang Li,Chubo He,Zhao Liu,Jiayao Shen,Yuqi Liu,He Li,Chengyi Zhang,Jian Liang,Ming Li,Chongming Gao,Fuli Feng,Ruiming Tang,Han Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RECAP框架，通过离线闭环优化流式结构化语义用户画像，在快手短视频推荐中实现了显著的推荐效果提升和用户使用时长增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言模型的用户画像生成器在开环中优化，能流畅总结历史行为但未直接针对改进未来推荐进行训练；短视频推荐中用户行为连续到达，画像需在有限容量下增量更新，缺乏有效的闭环优化方法。

Method: RECAP框架：1) 维护有界结构化记忆画像，结合LLM语义更新与确定性生命周期及容量控制；2) 构建画像定向语义反馈，通过LLM裁判过滤标签一致行为对，训练双塔评估器，其匹配分数作为GRPO奖励；3) 使用历史推荐反馈进行离线闭环策略优化。

Result: 快手短视频数据上，uAUC提升0.0084，Recall@2000提升约4.9%；七天在线A/B测试显示人均应用使用时长提升0.139%（统计显著）。

Conclusion: 反馈构建和策略优化的有效性得到验证，画像更新呈现更扎实的精炼和用户级抽象；RECAP框架为流式语义画像的闭环优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Language-based user profiles convert long behavioral histories into explicit semantic representations for recommendation. However, most profile generators are optimized in an open loop: they may summarize past behavior fluently, but are not directly trained to improve future recommendation. We study this problem in real-world short-video recommendation, where user behaviors continuously arrive as streams and profiles must be incrementally updated under limited capacity. This requires maintaining a consistent bounded profile state and constructing profile-targeted semantic feedback from industrial implicit behavior logs. We propose RECAP, an offline closed-loop framework for optimizing streaming structured semantic profiles with historical recommendation feedback. RECAP maintains each profile as a bounded structured memory by combining LLM-based semantic updates with deterministic lifecycle and capacity control. RECAP constructs profile-targeted semantic feedback by filtering label-consistent behavior pairs with an LLM judge and training a dual-tower evaluator whose matching score serves as a GRPO reward. Experiments on Kuaishou short-video data show that RECAP improves uAUC by 0.0084 and Recall@2000 by about 4.9% over the base generator. Further analyses confirm the benefits of feedback construction and policy optimization, and show more grounded refinement and user-level abstraction in profile updates. A seven-day online A/B test further shows a statistically significant 0.139% improvement in average application usage time per user.

</details>


### [78] [What Do Chinese-Language Generative Search Engines Cite and Surface? A Large-Scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15771)
*Tao Zhen,Yue Liu,Gege Zhang,Yixuan Niu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 对中国主流生成式AI搜索平台（网页端和App端）的大规模实证研究，分析了614个查询在8个平台界面的引用行为、来源归因、实体曝光和跨界面一致性，揭示了品牌选择率低、时效性依赖、信息不透明以及界面间系统性差异等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI问答系统正在改变信息访问方式，从排名搜索结果转向生成答案中的检索、引用和呈现，但目前对中文环境下生成式搜索的实际表现缺乏大规模实证研究。

Method: 对四个主流中文平台的网页端和App端共8个界面，设计614个查询，每个查询-平台-界面组合进行3次复制实验，构建了160,860条清理后的引用级数据集，分析引用行为、来源归因、实体曝光和跨界面一致性。

Result: 1) 品牌选择率仅8.3%，联系信息贡献率12.4%；2) 内容匹配度、跨来源出现次数和语义角色是重要预测特征，而5118-百度综合质量评分不是主要预测因子；3) 高时效性查询的引用半衰期约39天，低时效性约68天；4) 约13%的品牌曝光和71%的联系信息曝光无法匹配到对应来源；5) 同一平台的App端和网页端存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: 中文生成式搜索系统在信息选择、归因和呈现上存在选择性偏差、时效性依赖和信息不透明问题，且界面类型是分析的重要维度。

Abstract: Generative AI question-answering systems increasingly mediate information access, shifting content visibility from ranked search results to retrieval, citation, and presentation in generated answers. We conduct a large-scale empirical study of Chinese-language generative search across the Web and App interfaces of four mainstream platforms. The controlled design covers eight platform interfaces, 614 queries, and three replications per query-platform-interface combination. From 214,119 raw records, we construct a cleaned citation-level dataset of 160,860 records and analyze citation behavior, source attribution, entity exposure, and cross-interface consistency. Five findings emerge. First, brands in the citation pool were selectively surfaced in answers: the overall brand-selection rate was 8.3%, and 12.4% of retrieved sources containing contact information contributed contact information to answers. Second, content fit, cross-source occurrence count, and semantic role were relatively important in predictive models, whereas the 5118-Baidu Composite Quality Score was not the leading predictor for any examined outcome. Third, among cited pages with publication dates, fitted half-lives were approximately 39 days for high-timeliness queries and 68 days for low-timeliness queries. Fourth, approximately 13% of brand exposures could not be matched to the contemporaneous citation pool, and approximately 71% of contact-information exposures could not be matched to the crawled body text. Fifth, source sets differed systematically between the App and Web interfaces of the same platform. These results characterize how Chinese-language generative search systems select, attribute, and surface information and show that interface type is an important dimension of analysis.

</details>


### [79] [Scientific Claim-Source Retrieval Revisited: A Comparative Study of Style Transfer and Re-Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15875)
*Tobias Schreieder,Harsh Khandelwal,Yu-Ling Zhong,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对社交媒体科学声明难以验证的问题，研究了科学声明-源文献检索任务，通过对比稀疏与稠密检索模型，发现翻译为英语、融入元数据、风格迁移及验证推理重排序等方法可显著提升检索性能，最优模型MRR@5达0.758。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上的科学声明难以验证，容易导致 misinformation 传播，现有自动事实核查系统需要高效的科学声明-源文献检索技术，但声明与源文献在语言、风格和细节层面存在显著差异，使得检索面临挑战。

Method: 在CheckThat! 2026基准上对比稀疏与稠密检索模型；评估将声明翻译为英语、双语表示及融入元数据的效果；分析四种风格迁移方法；提出基于归因、实体重叠和验证推理的三种新型重排序模型。

Result: 翻译为英语的声明优于原始与双语表示；元数据带来额外提升；风格迁移对多数模型有效但最优风格因目标而异；验证推理重排序超越语义相似度，MRR@5达到0.758。

Conclusion: 验证推理重排序是最有效的策略，综合翻译、元数据融合和风格迁移可进一步提升科学声明源文献检索性能。

Abstract: Scientific claims shared on social media are often difficult to verify and may contribute to the spread of misinformation. To address this challenge, automated fact verification systems require scientific claim-source retrieval, the task of identifying the source publication underlying a given claim. However, claims often differ substantially from their source publications in language, style, and specificity, making retrieval challenging. We present a comparative study of scientific claim-source retrieval on the CheckThat! 2026 benchmark across sparse and dense retrieval models. Our results show that translating claims into English outperforms both original and bilingual claim representations, while incorporating publication metadata provides additional retrieval gains by capturing indirect source references. In addition, we analyze four style transfer approaches and find that they improve retrieval performance for most models, although the optimal style depends on the underlying retrieval objective. Finally, we investigate similarity- and signal-based re-ranking approaches, introducing three novel re-ranking models based on attribution, entity overlap, and verification-based reasoning. Verification-based re-ranking yields additional gains beyond semantic similarity and achieves the best overall performance with an MRR@5 of 0.758.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [CHRONO-RESOLUTION: A Dependency Resolution Dataset at Release Points for npm, PyPI, and crates.io Packages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15315)
*Imranur Rahman,Jill Marley,Ranindya Paramitha,Laurie Williams*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在指定发布点进行依赖解析的方法论，构建了包含npm、PyPI和crates.io三个生态系统的依赖解析数据集CHRONO-RESOLUTION，并 enriched with OSV漏洞数据，以支持软件生态系统演化分析和动态指标设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流包管理工具缺乏对历史时间点依赖解析的支持，导致无法在指定发布点分析依赖关系，限制了软件生态系统演化的研究和动态指标（如依赖新鲜度、依赖更新节奏）的设计。

Method: 提出一种在软件生态系统中构建发布点依赖解析的方法论，将该方法论应用于npm、PyPI和crates.io三个生态系统，并利用OSV漏洞数据对解析结果进行漏洞信息 enrichment。

Result: 构建了CHRONO-RESOLUTION数据集，包含npm、PyPI和crates.io三个生态系统中包在发布点的依赖解析结果，并集成了OSV漏洞数据。

Conclusion: 该数据集可帮助从业者和研究人员分析生态系统依赖图在发布点的状态，支持软件演化分析和动态指标设计，并已公开发布以供未来研究使用。

Abstract: Dependency resolution at a specified point in time in the past can provide insight into software evolution in software ecosystems and facilitate the design of dynamic metrics (e.g., dependency freshness, dependency update rhythm). However, dependency resolution at specified points in time is not possible in major software ecosystems due to a lack of support from package management tools. The goal of this paper is to aid practitioners and researchers in analyzing the state of the ecosystem dependency graph at release points using an enriched dataset with dependency resolution at release points for npm, PyPI, and crates.io packages. In this paper, we present a methodology to construct dependency resolution at release points of packages in software ecosystems, which we enrich with vulnerability data from OSV. We apply our methodology to construct CHRONO-RESOLUTION, a dataset of dependency resolution at release points for npm, PyPI, and crates.io packages, and release it for future research.

</details>


### [81] [Two-Path Status Verification for Outbound Enterprise Messaging Pipelines: Webhook and Scheduled Polling Fallback Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15529)
*Devam Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对企业外发消息管道中Webhook回调不可靠导致CRM状态记录过期的问题，提出了一种双路径状态验证架构：主路径通过实时Webhook触发异步事件更新，备用路径通过定时轮询任务直接查询提供商API进行状态调和，并采用幂等去重和故障检测机制确保最终一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递提供商返回的状态回调（Webhook）存在网络故障、端点不可用及提供商侧重试耗尽等问题，导致CRM系统中的消息状态记录永久停留在中间状态，亟需一种可靠的架构保证状态最终一致。

Method: 提出双路径状态验证架构：主路径为实时Webhook接收→REST端点→发布内部事件→异步更新记录；备用路径为可配置的定时轮询任务，检测持续处于过渡状态的记录，直接查询提供商状态API进行状态调和。同时引入幂等upsert实现去重，以及同步故障检测机制。

Result: 该架构解决了单路径依赖Webhook导致的消息状态永久未决问题，通过备用轮询路径确保了状态记录的最终一致性，同时考虑了多租户PaaS平台下的资源限制因素。

Conclusion: 双路径架构（实时Webhook+定时轮询）可有效克服Webhook回调不可靠带来的状态同步问题，为基于CRM的消息传递系统提供了一种兼顾实时性和可靠性的工程解决方案。

Abstract: Outbound enterprise messaging pipelines face a fundamental reliability challenge: delivery status callbacks (webhooks) from messaging providers are subject to network failures, endpoint unavailability, and provider-side retry exhaustion, resulting in stale status records in the CRM system of record. A naive single-path architecture that relies exclusively on webhooks leaves a population of messages permanently in an intermediate state when callbacks fail. This paper presents a two-path status verification architecture, generalized from patterns observed in production CRM-native messaging systems built on multi-tenant platform-as-a-service infrastructure. The primary path uses a real-time webhook received by a REST endpoint, which publishes an internal event for asynchronous record update.
