The fallback path uses a configurable scheduled polling job that detects records still in transitional status after a configurable interval and queries the provider's status API directly to reconcile state. We describe the event-driven primary path, the scheduler-based fallback, deduplication via idempotent upsert, the sync failure detection mechanism, and the platform resource-limit considerations that shape each design decision.

</details>


### [82] [Understanding Agent-Reactive Bugs at the Model-Harness Boundary: An Empirical Study of LLM Agent Issue Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15684)
*Jingyi Chen,Songqiang Chen,Hengcheng Zhu,Jialun Cao,Jiasi Shen,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM代理中的"代理反应式（AR）Bug"进行实证研究，分析了255个来自Codex、Gemini-CLI、LangChain和CrewAI的Bug报告，构建了包含可观察症状和触发LLM行为的双轴分类法，发现AR Bug常表现为无声错误、难以检测，并提出需要针对性的测试预言、复现支持和故障定位技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将LLM代理故障归因于模型能力不足或框架端缺陷，但忽略了LLM响应与代理框架交互时产生的Bug。这些Bug无法通过单独检查模型或框架理解，亟需专门研究。

Method: 对255个来自Codex、Gemini-CLI、LangChain和CrewAI的Bug报告进行人工分析，构建双轴分类法（按可观察症状和触发Bug的LLM行为分类），并分析用户提出的修复方案与开发者实际实施的修复方案之间的差异。

Result: AR Bug常表现为无声错误（缺乏明确测试预言），检测困难；LLM响应的随机性增加了Bug复现难度；用户倾向于建议框架端防护措施，而开发者可能将问题归因于LLM或对用户修复建议响应缓慢。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM代理特有的挑战，强调了设计测试预言、复现支持和故障定位技术的必要性，以帮助用户和开发者理解AR Bug的根本原因和解决方案。

Abstract: LLM agents span command-line interfaces (e.g., Codex) and agent frameworks (e.g., LangChain), integrating backend LLMs with harness code that parses model outputs, controls agent loops, and manages context. Both the harness and LLM-generated responses jointly shape an agent's execution. This architecture gives rise to bugs that cannot be readily understood by inspecting either component alone, because some bugs occur only when a particular LLM response elicits an abnormal reaction from the agent. Prior empirical studies of agent bugs have largely attributed failures either to limited model capabilities or to harness-side defects, such as outdated APIs and configuration misalignment, without characterizing these AR bugs. We conduct the first empirical study focused on agent-reactive (AR) bugs. Through manual analysis of 255 bug reports from Codex, Gemini-CLI, LangChain, and CrewAI, we construct a two-axis taxonomy covering observable symptoms and the LLM behaviors that trigger them. Our findings show that many AR bugs manifest as silent errors without well-defined test oracles, which makes detection difficult. The stochasticity of LLM responses further complicates bug reproduction. We additionally examine fixes proposed by users and implemented by developers. This analysis exposes a mismatch: users frequently advocate harness-side guardrails, whereas developers may attribute the issue to the LLM or respond slowly to user-proposed fixes. These findings point to the need for mechanisms that help users and developers understand the root causes and resolutions of AR bugs. Overall, the study highlights challenges specific to LLM agents and motivates the design of test oracles, reproduction support, and fault-localization techniques for AR bugs.

</details>


### [83] [Verified LLM-Driven Synthesis for Concept Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15718)
*Alcino Cunha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于概念(Concept)和反应(Reaction)的软件设计方法，给出了形式化语义支持安全不变量验证，并采用CEGIS风格、LLM驱动的合成流程自动生成满足不变量的反应设计。通过自然语言提示和正/负场景两种方式引导合成朝向用户意图，并提出了LLM驱动的场景启发技术支持设计探索。实验表明，仅靠不变量合成速度快但设计不一致，场景引导比自然语言提示更可靠，场景启发技术可恢复大部分设计但存在遗漏和非确定性覆盖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念设计将软件系统围绕用户面向的、自包含的功能单元(概念)进行结构化，并通过同步规则(反应)将概念组合成应用。现有方法缺乏形式化语义支持自动验证，且设计空间存在多个满足相同不变量的解，需要引导合成朝向用户意图。

Method: 1) 给出概念和反应的形式化语义，支持安全不变量自动验证；2) 提出CEGIS风格、LLM驱动的反应设计合成流程；3) 通过自然语言提示和正/负场景两种方式引导合成；4) 提出LLM驱动的场景启发技术支持早期设计探索。

Result: 在三个应用和十二个设计变体上评估发现：仅不变量合成速度快但设计不一致且有时不切实际；场景引导比自然语言提示更稳定地恢复预期设计，但最小场景可能导致过拟合；LLM驱动的场景启发技术能恢复大部分设计但存在遗漏和非确定性覆盖问题。

Conclusion: 不变量单独不足以约束设计任务，场景引导优于自然语言提示，场景启发技术有潜力但需解决覆盖完整性问题。

Abstract: Concept Design structures software systems around concepts: user-facing, self-contained units of functionality with a focused purpose. Concepts are composed into applications using synchronization rules called reactions, which specify how actions in one concept trigger actions in others. This paper first gives a formal semantics for concepts and reactions, enabling automatic verification of safety invariants in applications developed with this methodology. It then presents a CEGIS-style, LLM-driven synthesis procedure for generating reaction designs that satisfy such invariants. Because many different designs can satisfy the same invariant, we study two ways of steering synthesis toward the user's intended design: natural-language prompts and positive/negative scenarios. We also propose an LLM-driven scenario elicitation technique to support early design exploration. In an evaluation on three applications and twelve design variants using one LLM configuration, invariant-only synthesis reached verified designs quickly but often produced inconsistent designs across runs, some of which were implausible, showing that invariants alone underconstrain the design task. Scenario-guided synthesis recovered intended designs more consistently than natural-language prompting, although minimal scenarios can lead to overfitting. LLM-driven scenario elicitation, where the user classifies proposed scenarios rather than authoring them from scratch, recovered the intended designs in most variants when enough scenarios were elicited, but missed behaviors and non-determinism prevented reliable coverage in all cases.

</details>


### [84] [GapForge: Directed Compiler Fuzzing via Coverage-Gap Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15762)
*Mingxuan Zhu,Qingyuan Liang,Junjie Chen,Zhihong Xue,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GapForge是一种基于LLM的编译器测试生成技术，通过分析覆盖缺口、路径差异和覆盖反馈，在GCC和LLVM上显著优于八种现有技术，发现12个真实编译器故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代编译器代码库庞大复杂，现有测试生成技术忽略目标代码特性，导致大量编译器区域（尤其是边缘区域）测试不足，存在长期覆盖缺口。

Method: 三步方法：1) 基于覆盖得分的文件优先级排序；2) 对未覆盖行跨度与覆盖上下文进行路径差异分析，推断触发条件；3) 从需求和历史失败提示合成提示，利用覆盖反馈引导下一轮选择。

Result: 在GCC 14.3.0和LLVM 19.1.0上，72小时内核心模块覆盖率分别达68.13%和69.11%，比WhiteFox多覆盖24,736和19,798行代码；发现12个真实编译器故障（5个GCC，7个LLVM），包括8个崩溃和4个误编译。

Conclusion: GapForge通过细粒度、区域级别的覆盖缺口推理有效提升了编译器边缘区域的测试覆盖率，各组件均对最终性能有贡献。

Abstract: Modern compiler codebases (e.g., GCC and LLVM) are large and complex, making comprehensive coverage across diverse code regions highly challenging. Most existing test generation techniques ignore characteristics of the target code, producing test programs that exercise only a limited subset of it. Consequently, substantial compiler regions remain insufficiently tested, leaving persistent long-tail coverage gaps that survive across releases. Even existing white-box techniques achieve limited coverage on large-scale compilers. To improve compiler coverage, especially for hard-to-reach edge regions, we present GapForge, a targeted LLM-based test generation technique that reasons about coverage gaps. Unlike program-driven techniques that generate diverse inputs without modeling which regions they exercise, and unlike whole-file summarization that yields coarse guidance, GapForge treats coverage gaps as explicit region-level targets in three steps. First, it prioritizes files via coverage-driven scoring that favors large, undercovered files. Second, it pairs each uncovered line span with its enclosing covered context and performs path-difference analysis to infer fine-grained triggering requirements: the program structures and compilation options needed to reach the uncovered region. Third, it synthesizes prompts from these requirements and previously failed prompts, using coverage feedback to guide next-round selection. On GCC 14.3.0 and LLVM 19.1.0, GapForge significantly outperforms eight state-of-the-art techniques. Within 72 hours, it achieves 68.13% and 69.11% coverage on core compiler modules in GCC and LLVM, surpassing the white-box technique WhiteFox by 24,736 and 19,798 additional lines, respectively. Moreover, GapForge discovers 12 real-world compiler failures (5 in GCC, 7 in LLVM), including 8 crashes and 4 miscompilations, with each component contributing to its performance.

</details>


### [85] [Making Agent-Mediated Contributions Governable: A Project-Level Governance Manifest for Open-Source AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15769)
*Jinjin Gao,Luyang Li,Shufen Guo,Ligang He,Xiaoning Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"项目侧可治理性基础设施"理论，开发了智能体治理清单（AGM）作为双向治理合约。研究发现50个GitHub仓库缺乏项目级AI治理协调，AGM在评估实验中显著提升了风险标签恢复率（37/38 vs 15/37）和评审支持感知（6.14 vs 3.27），构建了包含可读性、可追溯性和可治理性的三层框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI和编码智能体加剧了开源软件治理张力：贡献生成速度远超维护者评估风险、证据和问责的能力。现有方案仅关注可读性和可追溯性，缺乏组织贡献级风险、证据、问责和评审关卡状态的项目规则。

Method: 对50个GitHub仓库进行诊断审计；开发Agent Governance Manifest (AGM)作为仓库托管的边界资源；进行15人参与的评审侧评估（75个任务级输出）和15人参与的贡献者侧可行性检查（45个任务）。

Result: 审计发现普遍存在治理工件和可读性，但缺乏项目级协调。AGM使精确风险标签恢复从15/37提升至37/38，评审支持感知从3.27提升至6.14（1-7量表）。贡献者侧45个任务中41个通过严格结构验证。

Conclusion: 构建了代理可读性、可追溯性和可治理性三层框架，将AI中介贡献理论化为可治理边界对象，推进了合规使能的数字创新治理，同时维护了维护者决策权威。

Abstract: Generative AI and coding agents are intensifying a central governance tension in open-source software (OSS): they scale contribution generation faster than maintainers can assess risk, evidence, and accountability. Existing responses improve agent-readability and traceability, but project rules must also organize contribution-specific risk, evidence, accountability, and review-gate states. We theorize this organizational arrangement as project-side governability infrastructure. A diagnostic audit of 50 GitHub repositories finds widespread general governance artifacts, observable agent-readability, and fragmented AI-governance cues, but no project-wide arrangement that coordinates shared rules, preparation obligations, verification rights, and maintainer decision authority across AI-mediated contribution workflows. We develop the Agent Governance Manifest (AGM) as a repository-hosted boundary resource and bidirectional governance contract linking contributor-side evidence preparation with maintainer-side verification. In a controlled reviewer-side evaluation with 15 participants and 75 task-level outputs, AGM-supported materials improved exact risk-label recovery (37/38 vs. 15/37) and perceived review support (6.14 vs. 3.27 on a 1-7 scale). In a contributor-side feasibility check, 15 participants completed 45 tasks; all final packages represented the core governance state correctly, and 41 passed strict structural validation. The study develops a three-layer framework of agent-readability, traceability, and governability, theorizes agent-mediated contributions as governable boundary objects, and advances compliance-enabling digital innovation governance while preserving maintainer decision authority.

</details>


### [86] [READU: Inconsistency-Driven Just-in-Time Detection and Repair of README Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15780)
*Doehyun Baek,Kilian Krampf,Michael Pradel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出READU技术，通过检测文档与代码或外部依赖之间的不一致性，实现README错误的即时检测与自动修复，在6000个提交中达到75%的精度，已确认44个错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级文档（如README）中的错误会导致用户运行时错误或调试时间浪费，而修复这类错误具有挑战性，因为文档混合了自然语言和代码，与源代码的关联松散，且发现错误后仍需人工修复。

Method: READU采用不一致性驱动的思路，通过高召回率提交过滤器筛选相关提交，并行运行内部一致性检查器（文档与源代码对比）和外部一致性检查器（文档与外部依赖对比），再通过警报判断器去除误报，最后自动合成文档补丁。

Result: 在Linux、Spring Boot等6个流行仓库的6000个近期提交上，READU检测到244个真阳性错误，精度达75%，平均每个提交消耗不到0.01美元和不到一分钟。其中217个错误被正确修复。已报告66个README错误，其中44个被确认，26个已修复。

Conclusion: READU能够高效、低成本地自动检测和修复README错误，为仓库级文档质量保障提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Repository-level documentation, such as READMEs, is often the first point of contact between users and a repository. When this documentation is incorrect, users may encounter runtime errors or waste their time debugging. We call such mistakes in repository-level documentation README bugs. Addressing README bugs is challenging because documentation mixes prose with code, its connection to the source of truth is loose, and finding a bug still leaves developers to craft a repair. This paper presents READU, an inconsistency-driven technique for just-in-time detection and repair of README bugs. The key insight behind READU is that README bugs often manifest as inconsistencies between documentation and another source of truth: either repository-internal facts, such as source code, or repository-external facts, such as external dependencies. READU applies a high-recall commit filter, runs internal and external consistency checkers in parallel, uses an alert judge to remove false positives, and automatically synthesizes documentation patches. On 6,000 recent commits from six popular repositories including Linux and Spring Boot, READU detects 244 true positives with 75% precision, while consuming less than $0.01 and less than one minute per commit, on average. Of these true positives, READU correctly repairs 217. We report 66 found README bugs, of which (so far) 44 are confirmed and 26 are fixed.

</details>


### [87] [In the Driver's Seat: A Multi-Company Study on the Reality of Autonomous Driving System Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15820)
*Qunying Song,Yuan Gao,Johannes Betz,Dietmar Pfahl,Mohammad Reza Mousavi,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对9家跨国公司的专家访谈，系统梳理了自动驾驶系统测试的工业实践、挑战与解决方案，并提出了一个以证据为中心的闭环测试框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统在真实世界中快速部署，但测试缺乏成熟的场景选择、性能评估和验收标准，亟需系统化理解当前工业实践与挑战。

Method: 对来自6个国家9家公司的ADS开发与测试专家进行访谈研究，采用主题分析法综合提炼工业测试实践、挑战、潜在解决方案和未来趋势。

Result: 当前实践以基于场景和X-in-the-loop测试为主，涉及多种工具、指标、基准和策略；主要挑战包括场景真实性、场景覆盖、仿真保真度和验收标准；潜在解决方案包括AI、世界模型和端到端方法；未来趋势是自动化、数据驱动和行业透明化。

Conclusion: 提出了一个以证据为中心的闭环测试框架，为ADS测试提供可操作的指导，并指出了未来研究和实践的重要方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems (ADS) are rapidly advancing and increasingly deployed in real-world applications. This creates growing demands for effective testing to ensure system functionality and safety. However, ADS testing remains complex and lacks well-established standards for scenario selection, performance evaluation, and acceptance criteria. To better understand current ADS testing practices and challenges, we conducted an interview study with experts working on ADS development and testing in nine companies from six different countries. Through thematic analysis, we synthesized industrial testing practices, challenges, potential solutions, future trends, and proposed an evidence-centered closed-loop testing framework for ADS testing. Our findings show that current practices primarily focus on scenario-based and X-in-the-loop testing approaches, supported by diverse tools, metrics, benchmarks, and testing strategies. The participants highlighted major challenges related to scenario realism, scenario coverage, simulation fidelity, and acceptance criteria, while also discussing potential solutions such as the use of AI, world models, and end-to-end approaches. Furthermore, participants envisioned future ADS testing to become more automated, data-driven, and transparent across the industry. Overall, this study provides a comprehensive industry-grounded overview of ADS testing, proposes an evidence-centered closed-loop testing framework to provide actionable guidance for ADS testing, and outlines important directions for future research and practice.

</details>


### [88] [Agentic Synthesis against Counterexample-Supplemented Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15854)
*Muness Castle,Eric Rubeck*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对补充反例草稿的智能体合成方法（agentic synthesis against counterexample-supplemented sketches），让编码智能体在发现领域规则后，通过显式审核反例来迭代修正代码草稿，保留策略演化历史并减少重复劳动。在CatSynth实验中，演化后的草稿在21个保留案例中通过了19个，优于回放所有案例的15个，且代码变更量更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在修复单个失败示例时，往往会破坏导致其失败的领域规则，导致后续迭代中重复出现相同的看似合理但错误的做法。需要一种方法让系统在实现过程中发现并保留正确的策略，而不仅仅是修复单个错误。

Method: 提出agentic synthesis against counterexample-supplemented sketches方法：人类提供部分代码草稿，编码智能体生成初始实现；当出现具体失败时，操作员显式审核并批准修正后的行为和规则；智能体根据单个反例修订草稿并修复/重新生成代码和提示；完整档案保留出处信息，每次修订前运行选定的回归测试集，定期进行干净的重新生成测试以验证演化后的草稿是否承载了学到的策略。

Result: 在CatSynth实验（使用GPT-5.4-mini）中：14个冻结候选案例中8个成为反例；三条路径均通过了8个已接受案例；演化草稿通过21个保留案例中的19个，初始草稿+回放所有案例通过15个；跨反例保留代码仅需9次Developer调用和719行累积变更，而回放所有案例需15次调用和2,394行变更，并通过18个保留案例。

Conclusion: 实验提供了可检查的证据，表明演化后的草稿承载了经过审核的策略，且保留代码减少了返工；但由于仅涉及一个模型和一种揭示顺序，结果不能证明该方法普遍优越性或超越编码检查的正确性。

Abstract: Coding agents can fix a failing example without preserving the domain rule that made it fail, so later generations can repeat the same plausible mistake. We present agentic synthesis against counterexample-supplemented sketches, a repository-native method for systems whose governing policy is discovered during implementation. A human starts with a partial, code-shaped sketch, and a coding agent generates the first implementation. When a concrete failure exposes missing or mistaken policy, an operator explicitly approves the corrected behavior and rule. The agent then revises the sketch and repairs or regenerates code and prompt surfaces for that one counterexample. The full archive preserves provenance; a selected regression set gates each revision before the next candidate is revealed; and periodic clean regeneration tests whether the evolved sketch, rather than prompt history or accumulated examples, carries the learned policy.
  We demonstrate the method with CatSynth, a synthetic browser application and captured coding-agent experiment. In one open-world run with GPT-5.4-mini, 8 of 14 frozen candidate cases became counterexamples. The rebuild controls inherited that promotion schedule, and all three paths passed the 8 accepted cases. Rebuilding from the evolved sketch passed 19 of 21 withheld cases, compared with 15 of 21 when rebuilding from the initial sketch and replaying all accepted examples. Retaining code across counterexamples required 9 Developer calls and 719 lines of cumulative artifact churn, versus 15 calls and 2,394 lines for replay-all, and passed 18 of 21 withheld cases. These results provide inspectable evidence that the evolved sketch carried reviewed policy and that retaining code reduced rework in this run; with one model and one reveal order, they do not establish general superiority or correctness beyond the encoded checks.

</details>


### [89] [Trans-Domain Digital Twin: Conceptual Foundations, Architecture, and Research Outlook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15908)
*Mansoorali Amiri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出跨域数字孪生（Trans-Domain Digital Twin）概念，作为复合/联邦数字孪生系统连续谱的操作化公式，通过对齐共享状态、耦合数据/模型/状态/误差/目标/控制、异构时间协调、联合决策与反馈自适应，连接异构领域孪生体，并给出七层概念架构、编排核心、合规条件、操作形式化、快-中-慢循环及离线训练/在线适应机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域数字孪生方法侧重于比较、重用、语义映射、标准化和互操作性，但未内在要求领域状态、误差、目标、约束、决策与控制之间的操作连接，亟需一种能实现领域间实质性操作耦合的公式化框架。

Method: 提出七层概念架构、跨域编排核心、最小合规条件、通用操作形式化、渐进式快-中-慢循环、单次离线训练+有界在线自适应机制，并给出概念验证与评估标准、成熟度模型、参考部署架构及运行时安全/溯源/版本/模型生命周期管理要求。

Result: 该框架可概念性映射至数字孪生、模型交换、分布式仿真及智能传感器标准，但其形式合规性与操作有效性仍需通过独立基准、不确定性量化、消融测试和现场验证进行检验。

Conclusion: 跨域数字孪生为异构领域系统的操作连接提供了系统化框架，但正式合规与操作有效性的严格验证仍是未来工作方向。

Abstract: Complex systems comprise heterogeneous domains whose states, uncertainties, risks, and control consequences can cross domain boundaries. Existing cross-domain digital twin approaches broadly focus on comparison, reuse, semantic mapping, standardization, and interoperability, but do not inherently require operational connections among domain states, errors, objectives, constraints, decisions, and controls. This article proposes the trans-domain digital twin as an operational formulation along the continuum of Composite/Federated Digital Twin Systems. This approach connects heterogeneous domain twins through an aligned shared state, explicit coupling of data, models, states, errors, objectives, and controls, heterogeneous temporal coordination, joint decision-making, and feedback-based adaptation. The proposed framework presents a seven-layer conceptual architecture, a trans-domain orchestration core, minimum compliance conditions, a general operational formalism, progressive fast-meso-slow loops, and a single-episode offline training mechanism linked to bounded online adaptation. It also describes conceptual validation and evaluation criteria, a maturity model, a reference deployment architecture, and requirements for runtime safety, provenance, versioning, and model lifecycle management. The framework is conceptually mappable to standards for digital twins, model exchange, distributed simulation, and smart transducers; however, its formal compliance and operational effectiveness must be examined through independent benchmarks, uncertainty quantification, ablation testing, and field validation.

</details>


### [90] [TARS: A Theory-of-Mind Agent for Personalized In-IDE Code Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15948)
*Leopoldo Todisco,Antonio Della Porta,Stefano Lambiase,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TARS是一个集成在VS Code中的LLM驱动的智能体，通过轻量级心理理论范式对开发者进行画像，并利用检索增强生成技术将解释锚定在项目文档中，实现自适应代码解释。实验表明，使用TARS的参与者完成任务速度提升26%，认知负担更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码理解是软件工程中最耗时的任务之一，但现有基于LLM的助手在生成解释时忽视开发者个体差异，且强制开发者采用破坏性的复制粘贴工作流，缺乏上下文感知的自适应解释能力。

Method: TARS集成在VS Code中，基于轻量级心理理论（Theory of Mind）范式对开发者的专业知识水平、角色和风格偏好进行画像，并通过检索增强生成（RAG）技术将解释锚定在项目文档中，实现解释深度和语气的自适应调整。

Result: 在有18名参与者参与的非平凡Java代码片段控制实验中，使用TARS的参与者任务完成速度提升26%，认知负荷更低，且认为解释能够根据个人画像进行有意义的自适应调整。

Conclusion: TARS通过心理理论驱动的自适应解释和RAG技术，有效提升了代码理解效率，降低了认知负荷，为开发者提供了更贴合个人需求的代码解释体验。

Abstract: Code comprehension is one of the most time-consuming tasks in software engineering, yet most LLM-based assistants produce explanations that ignore who is asking and force developers into a disruptive copy-paste workflow. We present TARS, an LLM-powered agent integrated into Visual Studio Code that supports program comprehension through autonomous explanations anchored directly to the code under analysis. Built around a lightweight Theory of Mind paradigm, TARS profiles a developer's expertise, role, and stylistic preferences, then adapts the depth and tone of its explanations accordingly, grounding them in project documentation via Retrieval-Augmented Generation. To evaluate TARS, we conducted a controlled experiment with 18 participants on non-trivial Java snippets. Participants using TARS completed tasks 26\% faster, reported lower cognitive load, and found the explanations meaningfully adapted to their profiles.

</details>


### [91] [DiffTestGen: Change-Directed LLM-Based Testing for Exposing Behavioral Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16024)
*Huimin Hu,Cristian Cadar,Michael Pradel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DiffTestGen是一种基于LLM的变更导向差分测试方法，通过静态调用图分析、项目文档和联合覆盖率指标，有效暴露代码变更引入的行为差异，在463个PR上达到78.2%的差异发现率和90.7%的联合覆盖率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在触发代码变更带来的行为差异方面效果有限，许多代码变更未能被有效检测，因此需要一种更有效的方法来暴露新旧版本之间的行为差异。

Method: DiffTestGen方法包含两个关键贡献：(1) 利用静态调用图分析和项目文档识别有效的测试入口点，引导LLM到达变更代码；(2) 提出联合覆盖率（union coverage）指标，结合新旧版本中修改代码的覆盖率，并通过向LLM提供针对性覆盖反馈进行迭代改进。

Result: 在463个PR的两个数据集上，DiffTestGen在78.2%的PR中暴露了行为差异，平均联合覆盖率达90.7%。相比基线方法，总共多暴露了99个PR，代码覆盖率分别提高了12.5%和15.6个百分点。集成Testora回归检测器后，能检测到现有最佳方法遗漏的回归缺陷。

Conclusion: DiffTestGen通过结合静态分析和LLM的迭代反馈机制，有效提升了变更导向测试中行为差异的暴露能力，并能用于检测回归缺陷，优于现有方法。

Abstract: As software evolves over time, it is important to ensure that any behavioral changes occur as intended by developers. A promising approach for this goal is to generate tests that expose behavioral differences between the old and new versions of a program. However, current approaches fail to trigger behavioral differences for many code changes. This paper presents~DiffTestGen, a novel change-directed, LLM-based differential testing approach specifically designed to expose behavioral differences introduced by a code change. The approach is enabled by two key contributions: First, DiffTestGen leverages static call graph analysis and project documentation to identify valid entry points for test generation and to guide the LLM toward reaching the changed code. Second, DiffTestGen iteratively improves our newly introduced union coverage metric, which combines coverage of modified code in the old and the new version, by providing targeted coverage feedback to the LLM. We evaluate DiffTestGen on two datasets comprising a total of 463 PRs. DiffTestGen exposes behavioral differences in 78.2% of the PRs while achieving an average union coverage of 90.7%. Compared with the baselines, DiffTestGen exposes 99 more PRs overall and increases code coverage by 12.5% and 15.6% percentage points, respectively. By integrating DiffTestGen with the Testora regression detector, we show that the identified behavioral differences can be used to detect regression bugs missed by the best existing approaches.

</details>


### [92] [What Does It Take to Research with AI? A Rapid Review of Competencies to Train LLM-Literate Researchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16083)
*Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro,Ronnie de Souza Santos,Rodrigo Siqueira,Breno Andrade,Rafael Batista Duarte,Gilberto Hida,Julia Alencar,Gustavo Pinto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该快速综述分析了40篇文献，识别出研究人员和研究生在科研中批判性和负责任地使用大语言模型所需的8项核心能力，其中领域专业知识与AI输出监督（n=123）最为关键，强调超越技术培训、以人类问责为核心的综合性能力培养。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在科学研究中日益普及，但研究人员和研究生需要哪些能力才能批判性、负责任地使用这些工具尚不明确，因此有必要系统识别和归纳这些能力。

Method: 采用快速综述方法，从Elicit和Google Scholar检索2022至2025年间194篇文章，经独立双人筛选后选取40篇进行能力提取和主题分析（Gwet AC1: 0.76–0.83）。

Result: 识别出8项能力：领域专业知识与AI输出监督（n=123）、元认知与AI使用决策（n=55）、伦理与学术诚信（n=53）、科研提示工程（n=38）、AI使用可重复性（n=29）、AI素养与技术知识（n=16）等；后者的缺失被视为风险因素。

Conclusion: 研究人员使用LLMs的能力培养不能局限于技术指导，而应围绕人类对知识产出的问责，整合认知、伦理和方法论维度的综合能力体系，对研究生课程和AI素养项目设计具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: The growing adoption of Large Language Models in scientific research has created a need to understand what competencies researchers and graduate students require to use these tools critically and responsibly. This rapid review analyzed 194 articles retrieved from Elicit and Google Scholar (2022 to 2025), from which 40 were selected for competency extraction and thematic analysis following independent dual screening (Gwet AC1: 0.76 to 0.83). Eight competencies were identified. The most prevalent was domain expertise and oversight of AI outputs (n = 123), encompassing subject matter mastery, systematic skepticism, source verification, and researcher accountability. Other key competencies include metacognition and decision making about AI use (n = 55), ethics and academic integrity (n = 53), prompt engineering for research (n = 38), and reproducibility of AI use (n = 29). AI literacy and technical knowledge (n = 16) was explicitly identified as a risk factor when absent, with domain expertise treated as a prerequisite for meaningful critical evaluation. The findings suggest that preparing researchers to use LLMs goes beyond technical instruction, requiring an integrated set of epistemic, ethical, and methodological competencies centered on human accountability for the knowledge produced. These results have direct implications for the design of graduate programs and AI literacy initiatives.

</details>


### [93] [Comparison of Energy System Optimization Software and Evaluation of Selected Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16121)
*Pedro Caixeta,David Gawron,Hüseyin K. Çakmak,Haozhen Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种能源系统优化软件（REMix、MTRESS、COMANDO、OEMOF、HOMER PRO），通过文献综述和对比分析，明确了各工具的最佳适用场景：REMix适用于投资路径优化，COMANDO擅长非标准组件系统，OEMOF是标准优化问题的首选开源工具，HOMER PRO适合快速综合优化且编程经验有限的用户，MTRESS因信息不足未完成分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源系统优化是实现碳中和的关键步骤，但面对多样化的目标和需求，选择最合适的软件工具十分复杂，因此需要系统性地评估和对比各工具的适用场景。

Method: 对五种预选软件工具（REMix、MTRESS、COMANDO、OEMOF、HOMER PRO）进行文献、文档、教程和示例模型的广泛回顾，制定对比标准并据此评估。

Result: REMix适合投资路径优化；COMANDO擅长含非典型组件的系统；OEMOF是标准优化问题的最佳开源工具；HOMER PRO适合快速综合优化及编程经验少的用户；MTRESS因信息不足未完成分析。

Conclusion: 不同能源系统优化工具各有专长，用户应根据具体优化需求选择最合适的工具，MTRESS的适用性有待进一步信息获取后才能评估。

Abstract: Optimizing energy systems is a crucial step toward achieving a carbon-neutral future, with software tools playing a major role in the process. However, selecting the most suitable tool for specific optimization challenges can be complex, given the diverse objectives and requirements of various energy systems. In this study, we aim to address this issue by evaluating five preselected software tools-REMix, MTRESS, COMANDO, OEMOF, and HOMER PRO-to identify the scenarios for which they are most suitable. To achieve this, we conducted an extensive review of literature, documentation, tutorials, and example models, and developed a set of comparison criteria that were subsequently used to evaluate these tools. Our analysis suggests that REMix is particularly effective for scenarios where investment path optimization is required. COMANDO excels in systems with atypical components. OEMOF is the best-suited open-source tool for standard optimization problems. HOMER PRO is recommended for users seeking rapid synthesis optimization, especially those with limited programming experience. Due to difficulties in obtaining sufficient information on MTRESS, we were unable to complete the analysis for this tool.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [GraphDx: A Cost-Aware Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Agent Framework for Sequential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15280)
*Shaoting Tan,Ning Liu,Yuntao Du,Shuyue Wei,Wu Shuai,Qian Li,Yanyu Xu,Wei Zhang,Lizhen Cui,Haitao Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphDx通过构建医学诊断知识图谱（MDKG）并引入三个协作智能体（感知、推理、决策），弥合了LLM在医学诊断中的知识-推理鸿沟，在MedQA和MIMIC-IV上将诊断成功率从50-68%提升至79-93%，同时将测试成本降低20-54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在顺序诊断任务中存在知识-推理鸿沟：虽然编码了丰富的医学知识，但在成本约束下难以进行系统推理，往往倾向于过度测试。

Method: (1) 自动化构建包含量化典型性、以动作为中心的拓扑结构以及双目标属性（诊断相关性和成本敏感性）的MDKG；(2) 三个协作智能体：感知智能体（语言理解）、推理智能体（在MDKG上执行确定性证据评分和成本感知规划）、决策智能体（语言生成）。

Result: 在MedQA和MIMIC-IV上，基于DeepSeek-V3、Kimi-k2、Llama-3.3三个LLM骨干，诊断成功率从50-68%提升至79-93%，测试成本降低20-54%。

Conclusion: GraphDx为自动化临床诊断提供了鲁棒、经济且可解释的解决方案，有效平衡了诊断准确性与资源成本。

Abstract: Sequential diagnosis requires balancing diagnostic accuracy against resource costs through iterative information gathering. Existing Large Language Model (LLM) approaches exhibit a critical knowledge-reasoning gap: despite encoding extensive medical knowledge, they struggle to reason systematically under cost constraints, often resorting to excessive testing. We propose GraphDx, a knowledge-enhanced framework with two core innovations. First, we design an automated pipeline that leverages LLMs to construct Medical Diagnosis Knowledge Graphs (MDKGs) with quantized typicality, action-centric topology, and dual-objective attributes for both diagnostic relevance and cost-sensitivity. Second, we introduce three collaborative agents (Perception, Reasoning, and Decision) where the Perception and Decision Agents handle language understanding and generation, while the Reasoning Agent performs deterministic evidence scoring and cost-aware planning on the MDKG. Experiments on MedQA and MIMIC-IV across three LLM backbones (DeepSeek-V3, Kimi-k2, Llama-3.3) show that GraphDx improves diagnostic success rates from 50--68% to 79--93% while reducing test costs by 20--54%, providing a robust, economical, and interpretable solution for automated clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [95] [Causal-Audit: Explicit and Auditable Graph-based Reasoning via Target-Aware Causal Chain Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15281)
*Su Lan,Xuefei Yin,Yanming Zhu,Alan Wee-Chung Liew*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种显式可审计的因果推理框架，通过构建目标感知因果图、模块化推理和路径级因果证据聚合，在无上下文干预问答任务中优于现有LLM方法，并提供可解释的推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法依赖隐式语言级推理，导致因果假设不透明、推理路径不可验证、在复杂干预场景下预测脆弱，尤其缺乏无上下文场景下的显式因果推理能力。

Method: 通过四个模块化阶段构建显式因果推理：1) 目标感知因果图构建（以目标变量为核心约束抑制无关变量和虚假关系）；2) 结构化因果推理；3) 路径级因果证据聚合（融合多条因果路径，建模增强和抵消效应）；4) 基于聚合证据的决策。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，该框架一致优于现有LLM方法，同时提供可解释和可审计的因果推理轨迹。

Conclusion: 显式因果图与模块化推理能有效提升LLM在无上下文干预问答中的推理鲁棒性和可解释性，路径级证据聚合超越了单链推理的局限性。

Abstract: Causal and intervention-based question answering is fundamental to advancing large language models (LLMs) toward reasoning beyond surface-level correlations and understanding underlying causal mechanisms. However, existing LLM-based methods often rely on implicit language-level reasoning, resulting in opaque causal assumptions, unverifiable reasoning paths, and fragile predictions under complex interventions, particularly in context-free settings. In this paper, we propose an explicit and auditable causal reasoning framework for context-free intervention-based question answering. Our method formulates causal inference as structured reasoning over an explicit causal graph through four modular stages, rather than implicit end-to-end prediction. A key innovation is a target-aware causal graph construction strategy that treats the target variable as a core constraint during graph expansion, effectively suppressing irrelevant variables, spurious causal relations, and reasoning noise. We further introduce a path-level causal evidence aggregation mechanism that combines multiple causal paths while modeling both reinforcing and counteracting effects, enabling robust decision-making beyond single-chain reasoning. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms existing LLM-based methods while providing interpretable and auditable causal reasoning traces.

</details>


### [96] [Cura 1T: Specialized Model for Agentic Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15314)
*actAVA AI,:,Haolin Chen,Leon Qi,Steve Brown,Deon Metelski,Tao Xia,Joonyul Lee,Qixuan Wang,Kevin Riley,Frank Wang,Weiran Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cura 1T是一种通过人类门控自我进化循环训练的医疗专用大语言模型，能够在患者咨询、临床推理、交互诊断和电子健康记录工具使用等多种医疗场景中达到前沿水平，同时保持通用推理和智能体能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗大语言模型要么局限于单一医疗任务，要么在覆盖多种医疗用例时面临能力冲突——针对某任务的优化会损害其他任务的表现。亟需一种能同时处理患者咨询、图文临床推理、交互式诊断和EHR工具使用等多种医疗场景的专用模型。

Method: 提出人类门控自我进化循环（Human-gated Self-evolution Loop）：每轮进化中，训练智能体规划目标能力→训练模型→评估基准轨迹→从观察到的失败中精炼数据混合策略。通过这种数据中心的循环机制，利用针对性合成数据和精选数据而非单次通用医疗数据更新来持续改进模型。

Result: 在医疗评估套件中，Cura 1T在多个基准上达到或接近最优性能；同时在领域外推理和智能体基准测试中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 人类门控自我进化循环是一种有效的医疗LLM训练范式，能够在不牺牲通用能力的前提下实现多维度医疗专业能力的协同提升，为构建覆盖完整医疗场景的专用模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Healthcare spans high-stakes communication, expert reasoning, and workflow execution, yet specialized LLMs that cover these use cases together remain limited. A healthcare model must handle patient consultation, clinical reasoning over text and images, interactive diagnosis, and electronic health record (EHR) tool use. These capabilities fail in different ways, and a narrow update for one task can degrade another. We present Cura 1T, a healthcare-specialized LLM trained through a human-gated self-evolution loop. In each evolution round, a training agent plans a target capability, trains the model, evaluates benchmark trajectories, and refines the data mixture from observed failures. This data-centered loop improves the model through targeted synthetic and curated examples rather than a single generic medical-data update. Across the healthcare evaluation suite, Cura 1T ranks at or near the top among frontier baselines, while remaining competitive on out-of-domain reasoning and agentic benchmarks.

</details>


### [97] [AnovaX: A Local, Multi-Agent Voice Assistant with LLM Planning, Typed Executors, and Adaptive Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15367)
*Raunak B Sinha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnovaX 是一个完全本地运行的桌面语音助手，通过 LLM 规划器、多智能体编排和自适应恢复循环，将桌面本身作为操作界面，支持语音控制应用、输入、浏览等操作，并可通过手机远程驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前桌面语音助手严重依赖云端管道，将原始音频发送到云端，且仅暴露固定的技能集，缺乏本地化、可扩展的灵活架构。

Method: 采用单一 Python 进程集成唤醒词门控、语音管线、LLM 规划器（Gemini，输出 JSON 格式工具调用计划）、白名单/黑名单安全层、多智能体编排器（将计划转化为带超时和重试策略的专用子智能体）、自适应恢复循环（ReAct 风格提示，通过只读工具的推测执行隐藏延迟）。MetaAgent 支持递归子目标委托（最多两层嵌套）。配套 Flask 服务器通过本地 WiFi 提供手机远程控制、实时事件流和 MJPEG 屏幕回传。

Result: 一个仅有几千行代码的桌面语音助手，能够打开应用、输入文本、运行搜索、协调并发操作、从单步故障中恢复，并完全通过手机远程驱动，无需 LLM 直接接触键盘。

Conclusion: AnovaX 证明了一个轻量级、可理解的本地优先架构足以完成日常桌面操作任务，无需与 Siri/Alexa 竞争，而是展示一种更自主、透明的语音交互替代方案。

Abstract: Desktop voice assistants are still dominated by cloud pipelines that ship raw audio off the machine and expose a fixed set of skills. We describe AnovaX, a small local-first assistant that runs entirely on the user's computer and treats the desktop itself as its action surface. A single Python process wires together a wake-word gate, a speech pipeline, an LLM planner (Gemini) that emits a JSON plan of tool calls, a whitelist-and-denylist safety layer, a multi-agent orchestrator that translates each plan into typed child agents on a bounded thread pool, and an adaptive recovery loop that takes over whenever a core step fails. Every tool corresponds to a specialized agent class (AppAgent, TypingAgent, BrowserAgent and six others) with its own timeout, retry policy, and shared-resource locks. A recursive MetaAgent lets the planner delegate a sub-goal back to itself, capped at two levels of nesting. The recovery loop uses a compact ReAct-style prompt and hides Gemini's latency behind speculative execution of read-only tools. A companion Flask server exposes a phone-friendly remote over the local WiFi, mirrors every agent lifecycle event to the phone in real time, and streams the laptop's screen back over MJPEG so the user can watch remote commands land as they run. The point of the project is less to compete with Siri or Alexa than to show that a legible, few-thousand-line assistant is enough to open apps, type into them, run searches, coordinate concurrent actions, recover from single-step failures, and be driven entirely from a phone in another room -- without the LLM ever touching the keyboard.

</details>


### [98] [Precise but Uncoupled: Reviewer Precision Does Not Guarantee Critique Uptake in Multi-Agent Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15388)
*Chih-Hsuan Yang,Jingyan Jiang,Vikram Vasudevan,Cheng-Hau Yang,Huihuo Zheng,Le Chen,Eliu A. Huerta,Venkatram Vishwanath,Ian T. Foster,Rajeev Thakur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在Omni-MATH难题中，广播式同行讨论优于规划-执行-评审流水线（PER），原因不在于评审者检测精度（PER更高），而在于PER的批评更难改变后续答案，导致修复效果差。评审检测质量与批评采纳是可分离的，单纯高精度评审不一定提升系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验数学/科学智能体系统中常见的层级化评审设计（即专门的评审阶段能够将错误候选转为正确候选）这一假设是否成立。

Method: 在4,181个Omni-MATH问题上，使用gpt-oss-120b作为演员，对比广播式同行讨论与规划-执行-评审流水线（PER）的性能；通过控制评审者精度、批评采纳率等变量，分析性能差异来源。

Result: 1) 协作在简单层级增益小，从第4层起增益显著；2) 广播式讨论在困难问题上优于PER；3) PER的评审者精度更高（0.861 vs 0.644），但其批评更难改变后续候选答案，导致修复效果差；4) 强制显式确认降低PER性能，将评审引导直接嵌入求解器工作上下文可部分改善但未消除差距。

Conclusion: 评审检测质量与批评采纳是经验可分离的；以评审者为中心的评价可能高估系统质量——协议可能很好地发现错误，但如果不能基于这些批评采取行动，仍无法解决更多问题。

Abstract: Many math- and science-oriented agent systems use hierarchical designs with specialized reviewer roles, assuming that a dedicated review stage should help turn wrong candidates into correct ones. We test this assumption on 4,181 verifier-grounded Omni-MATH problems using matched gpt-oss-120b actors. Collaboration adds little on the easiest tiers, but from tier 4 onward the gains open sharply; in this harder regime, broadcast-style peer discussion reaches higher final accuracy than a planner-executor-reviewer pipeline (PER). We ask whether this gap is explained by reviewer quality or by whether critique changes the next answer the protocol carries forward. It is not explained by reviewer precision alone: PER's reviewer is more precise than broadcast's (0.861 vs. 0.644), yet evaluator-verified useful critique is much less likely to change the next candidate and produces lower reviewer-guided repair. These results show that reviewer detection quality and critique uptake are empirically separable. Within matched PER interventions, forcing explicit acknowledgment lowers final accuracy, while embedding reviewer guidance directly in the solver's working context partially improves follow-through without closing the gap. Overall, reviewer-centric evaluation can overstate system quality: a protocol may spot errors well yet still fail to solve more problems if it does not act on those critiques.

</details>


### [99] [DrawingVQA: A Real-World Benchmark for Multi-Depth Visual-Textual Reasoning on Construction Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15418)
*Yoonhwa Jung,Junryu Fu,Mani Golparvar-Fard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DrawingVQA——首个面向真实建筑施工图纸的多模态大语言模型评估基准，包含33张施工图、92个问答对，覆盖三种推理深度和七个工程维度，实验表明当前模型与专家性能存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM基准多聚焦于自然图像或简单示意图，缺乏对建筑施工图纸这一融合抽象几何、符号标注、表格数据与领域文本的复杂视觉-文本领域的评估，无法衡量AI在真实工程工作流中的推理能力。

Method: 构建了包含33张真实"签发施工"图纸和92个专家标注问答对的基准，设计三层次推理深度（感知理解、上下文解释、领域专家推理）和双重分类框架（7个工程维度+4个MLLM能力维度），系统评估前沿MLLM性能。

Result: 当前最先进的MLLM在DrawingVQA上表现远低于专家水平，尤其在需要高层次领域推理的问题上差距最为显著。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了工程图纸领域MLLM评估的空白，揭示了当前模型在专业多模态推理上的不足，为未来领域专用多模态推理的发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce DrawingVQA, the first benchmark designed to evaluate multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on real-world construction drawings -- a core media in architecture, civil, and many other engineering practices. Unlike natural images or schematic floor plans, construction drawings fuse abstract geometry, symbolic notation, tabular data, annotations, and domain-specific text, forming a uniquely complex visual-textual domain core to engineering workflows. DrawingVQA bridges this gap with 33 "Issued for Construction" drawings and 92 expertly curated question-answer pairs, spanning three reasoning depths: perceptual understanding, contextual interpretation, and domain-expert reasoning. To evaluate model capabilities, we present a dual categorization framework to jointly analyze performance across seven construction-engineering and four MLLM capability dimensions -- the first to explicitly map engineering workflows to AI reasoning competencies. Evaluations of state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal a substantial gap between model and expert performance, particularly at higher reasoning depths. This benchmark lays a foundation for domain-specialized multimodal reasoning to allow for advancement on integration of AI-driven understanding and real-world engineering workflows.

</details>


### [100] [Do Coding Agents Need Executable World Models, Simplification, and Verification to Solve ARC-AGI-3?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15439)
*Sergey Rodionov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过消融实验分析ARC-AGI-3智能体各组件的贡献，发现完整验证变体在公开集上达到饱和（99% RHAE），但各组件效果因模型和推理设置而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前提出的ARC-AGI-3智能体集成了可执行世界建模、计划简化和精确回放验证三个组件，但各组件对性能的具体贡献尚不清楚，需要通过消融实验进行归因分析。

Method: 设计了四种嵌套的Codex智能体变体进行消融：文本基线、无回放验证的可执行世界模型、带计划简化的可执行模型、以及带简化+精确回放验证的完整变体。主研究使用gpt-5.4/gpt-5.5在高和极高推理努力下评估，后续使用gpt-5.6-sol进行探索性实验。

Result: 所有变体随模型增强和推理努力增加而提升；变体间差异小于预期；文本变体在两个gpt-5.5设置中优于可执行变体；简化在四个设置中的三个提升性能；完整验证变体在所有四个设置中排名第一但资源消耗更大；gpt-5.6-sol下验证变体完全解决所有公开游戏，RHAE约99%，动作数少于人类基线一半。

Conclusion: 完整验证变体在公开ARC-AGI-3集上已达饱和，但此结果仅反映公开集过拟合，保留集性能尚未测试；可执行交付物并非普遍有益，不同组件效果因设置而异。

Abstract: Our previous ARC-AGI-3 agent bundled executable world modeling, scheduled simplification, and exact replay verification, leaving unclear which idea accounted for its performance. We address this attribution question with four nested Codex-based agents: a textual baseline; a flexible-interface executable world model without replay verification; the same executable model with scheduled simplification; and a fixed-interface verification treatment that retains simplification and requires exact reproduction of recorded observations. The main study evaluates all four agents with gpt-5.4 and gpt-5.5 at high and xhigh reasoning effort on the public ARC-AGI-3 games. Exploratory follow-ups evaluate the textual and verification variants with gpt-5.6-sol at xhigh and max. The most robust result is that every agent variant improves with a stronger model and with greater reasoning effort. Within each model-effort setting, differences among variants are smaller than anticipated, while the effects of individual components vary across settings. Requiring a persistent executable deliverable is not universally beneficial: the textual variant outperforms the flexible-interface executable variant in both gpt-5.5 settings. Simplification improves performance in three of the four model-effort settings, with the weakest setting as the only exception. The complete verification treatment ranks first in all four settings, although it uses substantially more resources. In the gpt-5.6-sol follow-up, the verification variant fully solves every public game at both reasoning efforts, achieves about 99% RHAE, and uses fewer than half the total actions of the human baseline. Because the model postdates these games and held-out performance remains untested, this result should be interpreted as saturation of the public set only.

</details>


### [101] [Beyond a Joke: Multi-Angle Reasoning for Detecting and Explaining Harmful Humor in Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15442)
*Shanhong Liu,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh,Malika Meghjani,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAR-12框架，利用视觉语言模型从12个结构化视角解读模因，通过角色感知软门控注意力机制和原型分类器实现幽默与仇恨检测，在PrideMM和Memotion数据集上分别达到80.3%和75.9%的准确率，并生成可解释性强的推理结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网模因交织了视觉线索、文本内容和文化背景，尤其在幽默、讽刺和有害意图共存时难以解读；现有多模态分类器要么忽略这些相互依赖性，要么仅提供有限的可解释性，亟需能够提供可靠结构化推理的可解释模因理解系统。

Method: MAR-12框架首先利用视觉语言模型(VLM)从12个基于幽默与仇恨理论的结构化视角解读每个模因；然后应用角色感知软门控注意力机制学习每个视角的贡献权重；最后通过基于原型的分类器进行预测，并综合视角特定推理和注意力权重生成可解释性说明。

Result: 在PrideMM和Memotion数据集上，幽默检测准确率达80.3%，仇恨检测准确率达75.9%，均优于现有最先进方法；人工和GPT-4评估均确认MAR-12能生成连贯且有说服力的解释，尤其在幽默与有害线索共存的模因上表现突出。

Conclusion: MAR-12通过结构化多视角推理与注意力机制的结合，有效解决了模因中幽默与仇恨共存的复杂检测问题，同时提供了透明且上下文可解释的推理过程，在准确性和可解释性两方面均超越了现有方法。

Abstract: Internet memes intertwine visual cues, textual content, and cultural context, making them particularly challenging to interpret in scenarios where humor, sarcasm, and harmful intent coexist. These complexities highlight the need for explainable meme understanding systems that can provide reliable and structured reasoning to support both accurate classification and human interpretability. However, existing multimodal classifiers either overlook these interdependencies or provide only limited interpretability. In this paper, we introduce MAR-12, a novel framework that leverages Vision Language Models (VLMs) for meme detection and understanding in settings where humorous and hateful elements may coexist. The framework first interprets each meme through twelve structured perspectives derived from humor and hate theories. It then applies a role-aware soft-gated attention mechanism to learn how much each perspective should contribute, followed by a prototype-based classifier for the final prediction. Finally, explanations are synthesized using both perspective-specific reasoning and learned attention weights, ensuring transparent and context-grounded justifications. We evaluate MAR-12 on the PrideMM and Memotion datasets, where it achieves up to 80.3% accuracy for humor detection and 75.9% accuracy for hate detection, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches. Furthermore, both human and GPT-4-based evaluations confirm that MAR-12 produces coherent and persuasive explanations, particularly for memes in which humorous and harmful cues co-occur.

</details>


### [102] [From Black Box to Executable Logic: Explainable Reinforcement Learning through Prolog Expert Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15459)
*Eduardo C. Garrido-Merchán*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将深度强化学习策略（PPO）后验转换为可读、可执行、可编辑的Prolog逻辑程序，在有限MDP上提供可证明的回报损失界，在连续观测空间中以指数代价逼近任意精度，实验在离散任务上完美复现最优回报，在连续任务上部分恢复教师性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习策略是黑箱模型，缺乏可解释性，无法被人理解、被逻辑引擎运行、被优化器编辑。作者希望将其重写为可执行的逻辑程序，实现可解释、可验证、可编辑的决策系统。

Method: 三阶段后验转换方法：1) 提取冻结的PPO教师；2) 以经典关系学习方式从决策中归纳有序规则列表；3) 输出为Prolog程序并由现成逻辑引擎执行。后续扩展阶段编辑规则库，仅当策略评估确认回报提升时才接受编辑。

Result: 离散两室钥匙门任务（16,944个可达状态）中，扩展后的Prolog程序在每个种子下均达到精确最优回报，且在预算受限下10/10种子超过随机教师。连续控制任务中，Acrobot用11条子句匹配教师（噪声以内），CartPole恢复约97%回报，LunarLander仅部分恢复，符合指数下界预测。

Conclusion: 深度RL策略可以转换为可解释的逻辑程序，具有可证明的回报损失界和单调改进保证；但在连续高维观测空间中，转换面临指数级计算代价的根本性瓶颈。

Abstract: A trained deep reinforcement learning policy is a black box, and we ask whether it can be made explainable by rewriting it as an executable logic program that reproduces its behaviour and that a person can read, a logic engine can run, and an optimizer can edit. We present a three-stage post-hoc transformation that extracts a frozen proximal policy optimization teacher, induces an ordered rule list from its decisions in the manner of classical relational learning, and emits the result as a Prolog program whose every decision is executed by an off-the-shelf logic engine; a subsequent expansion stage edits the rule base and accepts an edit only when policy evaluation certifies a return increase. We prove four guarantees. A return-loss bound makes the distilled program a machine-checkable certificate in a finite Markov decision process, and the expansion loop improves monotonically and terminates. For the continuous-observation setting we answer whether the conversion is possible at all: the propositional threshold instantiation converts the network to arbitrary fidelity as the resolution B grows, with disagreement O(1/B) and a return gap that closes at the same rate, and a matching lower bound shows the cost is exponential in the observation dimension for an oblique decision boundary. Empirically, on a two-room key-and-door task with 16,944 reachable states the expanded Prolog program attains exact optimal return in every seed and, in a budget-capped regime, exceeds the stochastic teacher on exact return in ten of ten seeds. On three continuous-control tasks the emitted program substitutes the network, matching the neural teacher within noise on Acrobot with eleven clauses and recovering about 97% of its return on CartPole, while on the finer-control LunarLander it recovers only partially, exactly the ceiling the exponential lower bound predicts.

</details>


### [103] [A Critical Analysis of Trustworthy AI Tools, Mark Frameworks, and the Implementation Chasms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15480)
*Michael Papademas,Xenia Ziouvelou,Kostas Karpouzis,Vangelis Karkaletsis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于OECD数据集，对可信人工智能（TAI）的实践工具和信任标记框架进行了批判性分析，发现当前工具在伦理关注点、生命周期覆盖、利益相关者定位和工具类型上存在显著不对称性：过度聚焦公平性、透明度和鲁棒性，忽视可解释性、数字安全和环境可持续性；多数工具集中于AI开发后阶段，缺乏对早期设计阶段的指导；教育与政策参与严重不足。研究主张需扩展伦理目标、将伦理嵌入AI全生命周期，并推动更广泛的多方参与，以弥合AI原则与实践之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量高层次的AI伦理准则，但这些框架因缺乏具体实施机制而备受批评。本文旨在诊断当前可信AI实践工具的实施缺口，并推动从抽象原则向可操作实践的转化。

Method: 基于OECD提供的综合数据集，采用实证映射（empirical mapping）和描述性比较分析（descriptive comparative analysis），系统分析现有的TAI工具和信任标记框架。

Result: 识别出伦理关注点、生命周期覆盖、利益相关者定位和工具类型四方面的显著不对称性：公平性、透明度和鲁棒性受到过度强调，而可解释性、数字安全和环境可持续性被忽视；工具集中于后开发阶段，早期设计与数据收集阶段缺乏指导；教育与政策参与严重不足，TAI实践被行业内的技术和程序性措施主导。

Conclusion: 弥合AI原则与实践之间的鸿沟需要扩展伦理目标范围、将伦理考量嵌入AI全生命周期各阶段，并推动更广泛的多方利益相关者参与。研究既提供了现有实施缺口的诊断，也提出了推进更全面、包容和可执行的AI治理的可操作建议。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) systems increasingly impact society, ensuring their ethical and trustworthy deployment has become a global priority. While a myriad of high-level ethical guidelines have emerged, criticism persists that these frameworks remain abstract and lack concrete mechanisms for implementation. This paper conducts a critical analysis of tools and trust mark frameworks intended to operationalize trustworthy AI (TAI), drawing on a comprehensive dataset from the OECD. Through empirical mapping and descriptive comparative analysis, we identify significant asymmetries in ethical focus, lifecycle coverage, stakeholder targeting, and tool typology. Our findings show a strong emphasis on fairness, transparency, and robustness, with comparatively little attention paid to explainability, digital security, and environmental sustainability. Moreover, most tools and certifications concentrate on post-development stages, with limited guidance for early design or data collection phases. Educational initiatives and policy engagement are notably underdeveloped, suggesting that current TAI efforts are dominated by technical and procedural measures within industry contexts. We argue that bridging the persistent chasm between AI principles and practice requires expanding ethical objectives, embedding ethics across the AI lifecycle, and fostering broader multi-stakeholder participation. This study provides both a diagnosis of existing implementation gaps and actionable recommendations for advancing more holistic, inclusive, and enforceable AI governance

</details>


### [104] [Logic, Optimization, and Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15532)
*J. N. Hooker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了逻辑与优化相结合在基于规则的AI中的应用，探讨了多种逻辑体系与优化方法的协同，并强调这种结合在提升AI透明性（可重现性、可解释性、可信赖性和公平性）方面的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI透明性日益受到重视（涉及可重现性、可解释性、可信赖性和公平性），规则型AI因其天然透明性而成为解决方案之一。逻辑提供了规则编码和推理的媒介，而优化提供了计算推理的强大技术，二者的结合在现代先进优化方法的推动下变得愈加实用。

Method: 综述了逻辑-优化合作的多个领域：概率逻辑、贝叶斯逻辑、信念逻辑与Dempster-Shafer理论、非单调（默认）逻辑、多值逻辑、基于布尔回归的含噪数据逻辑公式推断。展示了如何使用决策图和基于逻辑的Benders分解计算投影（逻辑与优化的基本问题），描述了后最优性分析在解释结论达成过程以增强透明性中的应用，以及优化在回答集编程模理论中的角色。

Result: 展示了逻辑与优化结合在多种逻辑体系中的具体应用方法，提供了通过决策图和逻辑Benders分解求解投影问题的方案，阐明了后最优性分析对透明性的增强作用。

Conclusion: 逻辑与优化的结合是提升规则型AI透明性的有效途径，当前先进的优化方法使其变得更加实用，并指出了未来可能的研究方向。

Abstract: Logic and optimization can, in combination, make valuable contributions to rule-based AI. Logic is the obvious medium for encoding a rule base and drawing inferences from it, while optimization provides a powerful technology for computing inferences. Their combination has taken on new relevance amid a growing concern for transparency in AI. which is important for reproducibility, explainability, trustworthiness, and fairness. Rule-based AI provides a natural solution to transparency that is becoming increasingly practical due to today's highly advanced optimization methods. This article surveys several areas of logic-optimization partnership, including probabilistic logic, Bayesian logic, belief logics and Dempster-Shafer theory, nonmonotonic (default) logic, many-valued logics, and inference of logical formulas from noisy data based on Boolean regression. It shows how to compute projections, the fundamental problem of both logic and optimization, using decision diagrams and logic-based Benders decomposition. It describes the use of postoptimality analysis to explain how conclusions are reached, further enhancing transparency, as well as the role of optimization in answer set programming modulo theories. The paper concludes by suggesting possible future research directions.

</details>


### [105] [SeerGuard: A Safety Framework for Mobile GUI Agents via World Model Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15550)
*Xue Yu,Bo Yuan,Pengshuai Yang,Kailin Zhao,Hong Hu,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SeerGuard，一个面向移动GUI代理的后果感知安全框架，通过执行前指令筛选和动作风险评估来预防危险操作。基于统一的安全增强世界模型（SAWM），该框架能有效识别风险并提升代理安全性，实验表明在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上安全效用得分从0.191提升至0.596，风险成本得分从0.347降至0.130。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动GUI代理的安全机制主要反应式应对风险，缺乏在执行前评估风险的能力，导致单个错误操作可能引发不可逆后果。

Method: 提出SeerGuard框架，包含执行前指令筛选和动作级风险评估；通过多任务学习构建统一的安全增强世界模型（SAWM），整合语义下一状态预测与安全风险评估。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，安全效用得分从0.191提升至0.596（ω=0.8），风险成本得分从0.347降至0.130（α=0.8），且能有效跨不同移动GUI代理泛化。

Conclusion: SeerGuard通过后果感知的预防机制显著提升了移动GUI代理的安全性，SAWM在指令筛选、动作风险评估和下一状态预测方面均有效。

Abstract: Mobile graphical user interface (GUI) agents have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in automating complex tasks, yet they introduce critical safety risks where a single erroneous action can lead to irreversible consequences. Existing safety mechanisms are primarily reactive, lacking the ability to assess risks before execution. In this paper, we introduce SeerGuard, a consequence-aware safety framework designed to mitigate these risks through pre-execution instruction-level screening and action-level risk assessment. Specifically, the action-level assessment analyzes agent-proposed actions within current GUI states, anticipating likely outcomes to identify risks before they are executed. To enable these capabilities, we construct a unified safety-augmented world model (SAWM) via multi-task learning, integrating semantic next-state prediction with safety risk assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SeerGuard generalizes effectively across diverse mobile GUI agents. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, it increases the safety-utility score from $0.191$ to $0.596$ at $ω=0.8$ and reduces the risk-cost score from $0.347$ to $0.130$ at $α=0.8$. Further analyses on our SAWM validate the effectiveness of the instruction-level screening, alongside the capability of action risk assessment and next-state prediction.

</details>


### [106] [MGDT: MLLM-Guided Diffusion Transformer with Relation-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15592)
*Xu Hou,Meiyu Liang,Wei Huang,Yawen Li,Zhe Xue,Wu Liu,Guanhua Ye,Lei Shi,Kangkang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MGDT提出了一种"先对齐再扩散"的范式，通过关系自适应语义路由MoE模块筛选多模态特征、用冻结的MLLM进行语义对齐、再在统一空间中用图扩散Transformer生成缺失实体，在三个基准数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式MKGC方法直接在原始多模态特征上降噪，迫使去噪器同时完成关系相关的线索筛选、跨模态语义对齐和结构感知实体生成这三个任务，导致扩散条件含有噪声和语义不一致，性能次优。

Method: 提出MGDT框架，包含三部分：(1) RASR-MoE模块筛选关系相关的多模态语义变换路径；(2) 冻结的MLLM作为语义锚点，将路由后的多模态表示对齐到统一潜空间；(3) KGDT在对齐空间中进行图条件去噪生成。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: "先对齐再扩散"的范式能有效解耦MKGC中的多任务需求，提升多模态知识图谱补全的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion (MKGC) requires inferring missing entities from structural, textual, and visual cues. Existing diffusion-based MKGC methods usually denoise directly on raw multimodal features. Such a design forces the denoiser to simultaneously perform relation-dependent cue selection, cross-modal semantic alignment, and structure-aware entity generation, which introduces noisy and semantically inconsistent conditions for diffusion and consequently leads to suboptimal completion performance. To address this limitation, we propose MGDT: MLLM-Guided Diffusion Transformer with Relation-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts (MGDT), a novel MKGC framework built on an align-then-diffuse paradigm. MGDT first employs a Relation-Adaptive Semantic Routing Mixture-of-Experts (RASR-MoE) module to select relation-relevant multimodal semantic transformation paths and suppress irrelevant modality interference. MGDT then uses a frozen Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a semantic anchor to align the routed multimodal representations into a unified latent space and reduce cross-modal semantic heterogeneity. Finally, a Knowledge Graph Diffusion Transformer (KGDT) performs graph-conditioned denoising generation in the aligned space to produce the missing entity representation. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that MGDT consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [107] [Neuro-Symbolic AI for LEED compliance: Document-Centric Benchmarking, Deterministic Numeric Checking, and When Multimodal Hurts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15647)
*Aritro De,Juliana Felkner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索了一种神经符号管道，使用本地部署的4B参数语言模型（gemma3:4b）结合确定性数值检查器，对LEED v4.1 BD+C认证文档进行自动合规性筛查，在153个信用级别决策上达到67.3%的文本核心验证准确率，并揭示了图像输入降质、提示策略依赖通过率等关键失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEED v4.1 BD+C认证过程高度依赖人工审阅数百页项目文档，耗时且容易出错。研究旨在探索能否用小规模本地语言模型替代部分人工筛查工作，并明确符号组件与神经组件的合理分工边界。

Method: 提出神经符号管道：PDF对齐到LEED信用章节→信用感知关键词签名检索证据→本地4B参数语言模型（gemma3:4b）进行合规性验证→LEED特定数值检查器处理定量阈值。实验涉及四栋大学建筑（484份PDF，153个信用级决策），并进行系统消融实验比较不同模型大小、提示策略及图像输入分辨率的影响。

Result: gemma3:4b模型在文本核心验证中达到67.3%准确率，优于llama3.1:8b（50%）。确定性数值检查器将EA-p2从50%提升至100%准确率。完整神经符号配置整体准确率61.6%，低于最佳纯文本基线。低分辨率图纸图像（150-300 dpi）持续降低准确率。基于通过率的提示策略效果不同：文档丰富项目适合评分标准提示，文档稀疏项目适合思维链提示。

Conclusion: 小规模本地语言模型在LEED合规筛查中具有可行性，但存在提取失败和对定性类别过于保守的局限性。神经符号组合并未超越纯文本基线，提示策略需根据项目文档密度动态调整。该管道为LEED自动化验证提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: LEED v4.1 BD+C certification remains a document-intensive process that requires reviewers to read hundreds of pages of project evidence and apply credit-specific threshold logic by hand. This paper investigates whether small, locally deployed language models can perform meaningful screening of LEED documentation and how deterministic symbolic components should share that work. A neuro-symbolic pipeline is introduced that aligns project PDFs to LEED credit sections, retrieves evidence with credit-aware keyword signatures, verifies compliance with a locally hosted 4-billion-parameter language model, and applies a LEED-specific numeric checker to quantitative thresholds. Experiments on four university buildings (484 PDFs, 153 credit-level decisions) show that a 4-billion-parameter model (gemma3:4b) is the strongest text-only core verifier, achieving 67.3% accuracy and outperforming a larger 8-billion-parameter model (llama3.1:8b) in this task. The deterministic numeric checker corrects arithmetic errors on key quantitative credits, moving EA-p2 from 50% to 100% accuracy and improving several other credits when required values are reliably extracted. At the same time, the full neuro-symbolic configuration achieves 61.6% overall accuracy, trailing the best text-only baseline due to extraction failures and conservative behavior on qualitative categories. Systematic ablations show that adding low-resolution drawing images (150-300 dpi) consistently reduces accuracy, and that prompt effectiveness depends on the building's ground-truth PASS rate: rubric prompts perform best on documentation-rich projects, while chain-of-thought prompts perform best on documentation-lean projects. Within the specific scope of LEED v4.1 BD+C compliance verification over raw project documentation, this pipeline and its baselines provide an initial reproducible reference point for both accuracy and failure modes.

</details>


### [108] [ToolVerse: Unlocking Massive Environments and Long-Horizon Tasks for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15660)
*Shuaiyu Zhou,Fengpeng Yue,Zengjie Hu,Yuanzhe Shen,Chenyang Zhang,feng hong,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToolVerse是一个面向LLM智能体的强化学习框架，通过从近400个真实MCP协议中自动构建大规模可执行训练环境、基于工具依赖图的任务生成策略以及细粒度的Turn-Aware Relative Advantage算法，解决了智能体在复杂长程工具集成推理任务中的鲁棒性和有效性不足问题，实验表明该方法显著提升了LLM在动态环境中的长程工具使用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在紧凑、定义良好的场景中表现出较强的推理能力，但在面对大规模、多样、动态的真实世界环境时，由于需要无缝集成多种工具，其鲁棒性和有效性显著下降。作者旨在弥补这一差距。

Method: 1) 从近400个真实世界MCP协议（约4500个工具）自动构建大规模可执行训练环境；2) 提出基于工具依赖图的任务设计策略，采用动态解锁采样算法生成长程任务，构建GUST数据集；3) 提出细粒度的Turn-Aware Relative Advantage算法缓解长程智能体RL中的信用分配问题。

Result: 在多个智能体基准上的实验结果表明，该框架显著增强了LLM在长程工具使用方面的能力，实现了明显的性能提升，并在动态环境中展现出鲁棒的推理能力。

Conclusion: ToolVerse通过大规模环境构建、基于依赖图的复杂任务生成和优化RL训练算法，有效提升了LLM智能体在真实动态环境中进行长程工具集成推理的能力。

Abstract: While LLM agents demonstrate strong reasoning abilities in compact and well-defined scenarios, they struggle to maintain robustness and effectiveness when faced with large-scale, diverse, and dynamic real-world environments that demand seamless tool integration. To address this gap, we introduce ToolVerse, a comprehensive framework that scales up agentic RL environments and enables agents to perform complex long-horizon reasoning in Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) tasks. First, ToolVerse automatically builds the massive executable agent training environments from nearly 400 real-world Model Context Protocols (MCPs) that contain about 4500 tools. Second, we propose a task design strategy based on a tool dependency graph, utilizing Dynamic Unlocking Sampling Algorithm to generate long-horizon tasks, and produce GUST (Graph Unlocking Sampling Tasks) dataset. Third, to alleviate the credit assigment problem in long-horizon agentic RL, we propose a fine-grained Turn-Aware Relative Advantage algorithm. We conduct extensive Agentic RL training using ToolVerse and evaluate our framework on serveral agentic benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework significantly strengthens LLMs' capabilities in long-horizon tool use, achieving a marked performance boost and showcasing robust reasoning within dynamic environments.

</details>


### [109] [S1-Omni: A Unified Multimodal Reasoning Model for Scientific Understanding, Prediction, and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15686)
*Jiahao Zhao,Junyi Liu,Lifeng Xu,Nan Xu,Qingli Wang,Qingxiao Li,Tianle Chen,Xiaoyu Wu,Yawen Zheng,Zikai Wang,Guanming Liu,Hequn Zhou,Jingyi Wang,Jingyuan Shu,Keqi Wang,Li He,Songyang Diao,Wenhui Xu,Xinyu Ren,Yaqin Fan,Yujin Zhou,Zhanao Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: S1-Omni是一个统一的多模态科学推理模型，能够同时处理科学数据的理解、预测和生成任务。它通过统一表示空间（涵盖CIF、SMILES、蛋白质序列、光谱、科学图像等）、融合科学定律与专家知识、以及任务特定解码三大组件，在200个科学任务上训练，在60多个基准上优于GPT-5.5和Gemini-3.1-Pro，部分任务达到甚至超越领域专用模型水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI for Science模型能力高度碎片化，领域专用模型、工具增强型LLM和科学语言模型各自为政，缺乏对异构数据、科学定律和专家知识的联合建模能力，限制了科学推理的整体性与泛化性。

Method: 架构三组件：(1) 统一表示：将自然语言指令与CIF、SMILES、蛋白质序列、光谱、科学图像等映射到共享表示空间；(2) 自然世界知识对齐：将科学定律和专家知识融入数据构建与训练，使模型基于科学证据推理；(3) 任务特定解码：支持性质预测、光谱到分子生成、蛋白质位点与结构预测、科学图像生成与编辑等多种任务。模型在S1-Omni-Corpus（200个科学任务，百万级推理样本）上训练。

Result: 在60多个科学基准上评估，S1-Omni在大多数基准上超越GPT-5.5和Gemini-3.1-Pro，在多个基准上达到或超越领域专用模型水平。

Conclusion: S1-Omni为统一的科学建模提供了一条实用路径，证明了将异构科学数据、科学定律与多任务解码整合到单一模型中的可行性。

Abstract: We present S1-Omni, a unified multimodal reasoning model for scientific understanding, prediction, and generation. AI for Science (AI4S) has advanced significantly through domain-specific models, tool-augmented LLMs, and scientific language models. However, model capabilities remain highly fragmented, limiting the joint modeling of heterogeneous data, scientific laws, and expert knowledge. S1-Omni addresses this gap by consolidating these capabilities into a single, coherent scientific reasoning model. The architecture of S1-Omni is built upon three core components: unified representation of scientific data, natural-world knowledge alignment, and decoding for domain-specific tasks. First, S1-Omni maps natural-language instructions and scientific objects, including CIF, SMILES, protein sequences, spectra, and scientific images, into a shared representation space. Second, it incorporates scientific laws and expert knowledge into data construction and training, enabling the model to reason from scientific evidence. Third, it performs task-specific decoding to support a broad range of applications, including property prediction, spectrum-to-molecular generation, protein site and structure prediction, and scientific image generation and editing. S1-Omni is trained on S1-Omni-Corpus, which covers 200 scientific tasks and contains millions of reasoning samples, and is evaluated on over 60 scientific benchmarks. It outperforms GPT-5.5 and Gemini-3.1-Pro on most benchmarks and matches or surpasses domain-specific models on several benchmarks. Overall, S1-Omni provides a practical path toward unified scientific modeling.

</details>


### [110] [Behavioral Controllability of Agentic Models for Information Extraction: From Fixed Workflows to Reflective Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15715)
*Lujia Zhang,Xingzhou Chen,Hongwei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM代理组件（如反思、记忆）是否能为信息提取任务带来可观察和可控的改进，通过在会议论文数据集提取任务中比较固定工作流、反思代理变体和优化代理条件，重点分析过程级行为变化和失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM代理被越来越多地用于复杂信息提取任务，但反思和记忆等代理组件是否真的能带来可观察、可控的改进尚不清楚，需要系统性地进行量化评估。

Method: 以会议论文数据集提取为任务场景，比较固定工作流基线、反思代理变体以及优化代理条件（S2，引入更丰富的PDF工具和动态工具选择），重点评估过程级行为指标（工具执行、重试、反思、内存使用、运行时间、故障恢复），将提取覆盖率和字段完整性作为辅助结果指标。

Result: 揭示了代理机制何时改变系统行为、这些变化是否提升任务完成度，以及观察到的失败模式如何启发在相同评估框架下的优化代理设计。

Conclusion: 代理组件的引入会改变系统行为，但并非所有变化都带来正向收益；失败模式的分析可用于指导代理设计的优化。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used for complex information-extraction tasks, yet it remains unclear whether agentic components such as reflection and memory lead to observable and controllable improvements over fixed LLM workflows. We study this question through conference-paper dataset extraction, where a system must identify datasets mentioned in scholarly PDFs and produce structured records. We compare a fixed workflow baseline with reflective agent variants and specify an optimized agent condition (S2) that extends the same task with richer PDF tools and dynamic tool selection. Our evaluation emphasizes process-level behavior--including tool execution, retries, reflection, memory use, runtime, and failure recovery--while treating extraction coverage and field completeness as secondary outcome measures. The paper characterizes when agentic mechanisms change system behavior, whether these changes improve task completion, and how the observed failure modes motivate an optimized agent design under the same evaluation harness.

</details>


### [111] [NeurOWL: An LLM-Based Neural-symbolic Framework for Incomplete OWL Ontology Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15776)
*Hui Yang,Jiaoyan Chen,Yiping Song,Renate Schmidt,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 NeurOWL，一个端到端的神经符号框架，用于在不完整OWL本体中验证语义合理的子类关系并提供逻辑解释，综合了子类验证与本体 abduction 任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的本体通常是不完整的，这给基于OWL本体的语义推理带来了挑战。现有方法难以同时处理子类关系的验证与缺失公理的推导。

Method: 提出 NeurOWL，一个端到端神经符号框架，联合利用形式语义（通过本体嵌入）和文本语义（通过大语言模型）进行子类关系验证与 abduction 推理。

Result: 在多个领域的真实本体上进行了评估，实验表明 NeurOWL 在不同领域均展现出强且鲁棒的性能。

Conclusion: NeurOWL 有效统一了子类验证与本体 abduction 任务，无需预定义缺失公理候选集，为不完整本体下的推理问题提供了可行的神经符号解决方案。

Abstract: OWL ontologies provide a formal knowledge representation framework that enables semantic reasoning, and have been widely adopted across domains such as healthcare and bioinformatics. In practice, however, real-world ontologies are often incomplete, which pose challenges for reasoning. In this work, we focus on a fundamental subsumption reasoning problem: given an incomplete ontology and a candidate (non-entailed) subsumption, determine whether the subsumption is semantically plausible and, if so, providing a logically sound explanation containing potential missing axioms. This task unifies subsumption verification with ontology abduction, and generalizes the latter by removing the need for a predefined candidate set of missing axioms. To address this subsumption reasoning problem, we propose NeurOWL, an end-to-end neuro-symbolic framework that jointly performs verification and abduction, leveraging both formally defined semantics and textual semantics through Large Language Models and ontology embeddings. We evaluate NeurOWL on real-world ontologies across multiple domains, demonstrating strong and robust performance across different domains.

</details>


### [112] [AgentFAIR: A Multi-Agent Collaborative Framework for FAIRness Evaluation of Geospatial Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15781)
*Ming Chen,Pranav Pai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentFAIR是一个多智能体框架，结合结构化元数据提取和13个LLM评估器，用于评估地理空间数据集的FAIR合规性，在50个数据集上平均得分为79.7%（可发现性）、70.4%（可访问性）、45.3%（互操作性）和72.0%（可重用性），与现有工具排名相关性0.31-0.61，子原则一致性89%，专家研究Fleiss' kappa为0.71，每次API成本约0.054美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FAIR合规评估工具评分标准不一、证据来源不同，在JavaScript渲染页面或特定仓库标识符上可能失效，导致跨工具评分差异大（标准差平均15个百分点，最高达30.3），需要更一致、可审计的评估方法。

Method: 提出AgentFAIR多智能体框架：结构化元数据提取模块 + 13个对应FAIR子原则的LLM评估器（每个输出0-3成熟度分数、引用证据和推荐），另设评论智能体检查证据一致性和逻辑，可触发针对性重评估。

Result: 4个基准工具排名相关性0.31-0.61，FAIR-enough比较无统计显著性；10数据集重复运行子原则一致性89%（标准差3pp），无评论时为71%；初步专家研究Fleiss' kappa 0.71，与专家共识对齐82%；API成本约0.054美元/数据集。

Conclusion: AgentFAIR在可审计性和可行性上表现支持其应用价值，但受限于基准规模有限、消融实验不完整及单一模型族验证，准确性和泛化性的结论需谨慎对待。

Abstract: Geospatial datasets support applications from urban planning to climate modeling, yet consistent assessment of FAIR compliance is difficult. Existing evaluators use different rubrics and evidence sources and may fail on JavaScript-rendered pages or repository-specific identifiers. For 50 datasets from 10 repositories, the standard deviation of normalized scores across available tools averages 15.0 percentage points and reaches 30.3 for one dataset. Because these outputs are not equivalent measurements, we use them to characterize disagreement and failure modes, not comparative accuracy. We present AgentFAIR, a multi-agent framework combining structured metadata extraction with 13 sub-principle-specific LLM evaluators. Each produces a 0-3 maturity score, cited evidence, and recommendations; a critic checks evidence and consistency and can request targeted re-evaluation. Mean Findability, Accessibility, Interoperability, and Reusability scores are 79.7%, 70.4%, 45.3%, and 72.0%. Rank correlations with four baseline tools range from 0.31 to 0.61; the FAIR-enough comparison is not statistically significant. On a 10-dataset repeated-run subset, sub-principle agreement averages 89% (standard deviation: 3 percentage points), versus 71% without the critic. A preliminary 15-dataset expert study yields Fleiss' kappa of 0.71 and 82% alignment with expert consensus. API cost is approximately USD 0.054 per dataset. These results support auditability and feasibility, while the limited benchmark, incomplete ablations, and single-model-family validation constrain claims about accuracy and generalization.

</details>


### [113] [Knowledge-Centric Agents for Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15845)
*Zhendong Li,Lei Sun,Ruibo Ming,He Zhang,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种知识中心化的框架，通过知识反转、注入和推理三个步骤，从真实工作流中提取层次化知识并注入模型，实现从任务描述到可执行工作流的生成，在节点多样性、结构连贯性和执行成功率上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法将工作流生成视为直接的文本到JSON生成任务，存在结构脆弱性且缺乏设计所需的经验知识，无法有效建模知识的结构、层次和推理动态。

Method: 提出知识中心化框架，包含三个核心步骤：(1) 知识反转——从真实工作流中提取层次化表示（伪代码、骨架、高层策略）；(2) 知识注入——通过监督微调使模型学习从任务到策略再到可执行结构的推理；(3) 推理阶段——可逆推理合成可执行工作流，并辅以自优化保证结构连贯性。

Result: 该方法生成的工作流在节点多样性、结构连贯性和执行成功率上均优于现有系统。

Conclusion: 为知识驱动的智能体工作流生成建立了新的基础，表明显式建模知识层次与推理动态是提升工作流生成质量的关键。

Abstract: Workflow generation in visual creation systems such as ComfyUI demands not only syntactic accuracy but also expert-level reasoning over modular compositions. Existing large language model (LLM) approaches often treat this as a direct text-to-JSON generation task, struggling with structural brittleness and lacking the experiential knowledge required for effective design. We argue that successful workflow generation requires modeling knowledge itself, including its structure, hierarchy, and reasoning dynamics. To this end, we propose a knowledge-centric framework that learns to invert, inject, and infer with knowledge across multiple abstraction levels. We first perform knowledge inversion to distill hierarchical representations, ranging from full pseudo-codes and skeletons to high-level strategies, from large collections of real-world workflows. We then conduct knowledge injection through supervised fine-tuning, teaching the model to reason from task descriptions to strategies and from strategies to executable structures. During inference, the model performs reversible reasoning to synthesize executable workflows, augmented by self-refinement for structural coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces workflows with richer node diversity, more coherent structures, and higher execution success rates than existing systems, establishing a new foundation for knowledge-driven, agentic workflow generation.

</details>


### [114] [DSWorld: A Data Science World Model for Efficient Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15901)
*Zherui Yang,Fan Liu,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出数据科学世界模型DSWorld，通过预测数据科学操作的环境状态转移来替代昂贵的试错执行，利用结构化状态构建、成本感知路由、轻量级执行和LLM模拟器，在RL智能体训练中实现约14倍加速，搜索推理加速3-6倍，转移预测任务比最强LLM基线高35.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主数据科学智能体依赖试错工作流，涉及大量昂贵计算，亟需一种能够在实际执行前预测数据科学操作效果的模型，以降低计算成本。

Method: 提出DSWorld框架，包括结构化状态构建、成本感知路由、轻量级真实执行和基于LLM的模拟器用于昂贵操作；构建8K规模转移轨迹数据集；提出反思性世界模型优化（RWMO）的纠错强化学习策略。

Result: DSWorld在RL智能体训练中实现约14倍加速，搜索推理加速约3-6倍，同时保持竞争性性能；在转移预测任务上比最强LLM基线高出35.6%。

Conclusion: DSWorld通过世界模型有效预测数据科学操作的环境状态转移，大幅加速智能体训练和推理过程，验证了该方法在降低计算开销方面的有效性。

Abstract: Despite strong capabilities in data understanding and decision-making, autonomous data science agents still heavily rely on trial-and-error workflows that involve expensive computation. This bottleneck motivates models that can anticipate the effects of data science operations before real execution. In this paper, we introduce the concept of Data Science World Model, which model the data science execution environment by predicting environment state transitions conditioned on current workflow states and candidate operations. We further propose DSWorld, a practical framework that combines structured state construction, cost-aware routing, lightweight real execution, and an LLM-based simulator for expensive operations. To support training, we construct an 8K-scale transition trajectory dataset and introduce Reflective World Model Optimization, an error-aware reinforcement learning strategy for improving transition prediction. Experiments show that DSWorld accelerates RL-based agent training by approximately $14\times$ and search-based inference by approximately $3$-$6\times$ while maintaining competitive performance, and outperforms the strongest LLM baseline by 35.6% on transition prediction tasks. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DSWorld.

</details>


### [115] [A Formally Grounded ODRL Evaluator: Implementation and Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15987)
*Jaime Osvaldo Salas,Paolo Pareti,Adeel Aslam,Christopher Maidens,George Konstantinidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对ODRL策略语言缺乏形式化语义导致互操作性差的问题，提出了首个支持所有规则类型、具有透明形式语义的ODRL评估器，在访问控制和监控场景下（包括静态和流式环境）实现了高效策略评估，并通过实验验证了其性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ODRL策略语言虽已成为欧洲数据空间中数据访问、AI治理策略和数据工作流的事实标准，但缺乏数学形式语义来指导系统实现策略评估，导致各类系统与工具对语言进行不同解释，限制了互操作性且无法保证结果的一致性。

Method: 基于已有的ODRL语义模型，形式化定义了访问控制和监控场景下（静态和流式环境）的ODRL评估问题；提出了一种新的高效算法并实现；通过实验测量性能，分析策略复杂度和数据规模等可扩展性维度；与现有ODRL评估器进行对比审查。

Result: 实现了首个支持所有规则类型且具有透明形式语义的ODRL评估器，并通过实验测量了其在策略复杂度和数据规模等不同可扩展性维度下的性能表现，与现有评估器的对比审查揭示了支持功能与评估模式的差异。

Conclusion: 本文通过形式化语义定义和高效算法实现，解决了ODRL评估中互操作性不足和结果不一致的核心问题，为ODRL在数据空间中的可靠部署提供了基础。

Abstract: The ODRL policy language is emerging as the de-facto standard for policy modelling data access and usage preferences, AI governance policies and data workflows in European dataspaces. The current standard has no mathematical formal semantics to describe how a system should implement policy evaluation. This has resulted in a variety of systems and tools that implement their own interpretation of the language, which limits interoperability and cannot guarantee consistent results. Based on an existing semantic model of ODRL, we formalise the problems of ODRL evaluation for the access control and monitoring scenarios, in both static and streaming settings, and we provide a novel, efficient algorithm and implementation. We present the first ODRL Evaluator with transparent formal semantics and supporting all rule types. We experimentally measure its performance, analysing different scalability dimensions related to policy complexity and size of the data on which a policy is evaluated. We compare our system with the state-of-the-art by providing a comparative review of existing ODRL evaluators, which highlights the differences in supported ODRL features and evaluation modes.

</details>


### [116] [Closing the AI Trust Gap: The Case for Independent Certification for Trustworthy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15992)
*Trisevgeni Papakonstantinou,Cansu Canca,Farah Nanji,Waheedullah Pardess,Jen Weedon,Jasmijn Remmers,Eliza Krigman,Matthew Ball,Yalda Daryani,Kiran Iqbal,Francielle Vargas,María Llorente Sánchez,Joe Humphreys,Fendi Tsim,Kelly Fitzpatrick,Jeff Dunn,Catherine Feldman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前负责任AI（RAI）实践虽多，但缺乏市场对可信赖性的认可，形成了"信任缺口"。作者认为问题源于过度关注内部过程（RAI）而非可独立验证的实际结果（可信AI），并提出独立的、以结果为导向的认证体系作为弥合这一缺口的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前负责任AI实践无法产生外部可识别、可验证的可信赖信号，导致信任缺口——负责任开发发生在组织内部，但消费者、监管者和股东无法区分真正可信赖的系统与仅满足最低合规要求的系统。

Method: 通过分析三项结构性失败（市场无法区分可信系统与仿冒品、评估针对模型和输出而非部署的社会技术系统、测量生态偏向避免伤害而非展示益处），并对比医疗、可持续性和安全领域的认证制度，论证现有AI治理工具缺乏整合治理基线、独立验证的积极结果证据和市场信号于一体的框架。

Result: 现有AI治理工具均未整合治理基线、独立验证的积极结果证据和市场信号三大要素；信任缺口因关注"过程"（负责任AI）而非"结果"（可信AI）而持续存在。

Conclusion: 提出独立的、以结果为导向的认证作为连接层，通过使可信赖性变得可测量、可比较并可在商业上获得回报，来弥合信任缺口，补充现有法规和内部治理机制。
